A Sphinx is a [[Mythological|Mythological Creature]] that can be spawned from [[Griffin Burrows]] during the [[Mythological Ritual]].
## Spawning
A Sphinx has a chance to spawn when digging [[Griffin Burrows]] with a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] or higher [[Griffin Pet]] equipped.
## Variants
|Level|Name|[[Griffin Pet]]; [[Rarity]]|Spawn Chance|Stats [[Health]]|Stats [[Damage]]|Drops Combat XP ([[Combat]])|Drops [[Experience]]|Drops [[Coins]]|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv315]|Exalted Sphinx|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|1.30% (1 in 76.67 mobs)|40,000,000|3,500|472|346|105|
|[Lv349]|Empyrean Sphinx|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|1.30% (1 in 77.07 mobs)|65,000,000|5,000|523|383|116|
[[Tracking]] increases a chance to spawn Sphinx.
## Behavior
When spawned, the Sphinx will ask the player a question in chat. Answering correctly rewards the player with a random piece of [[Griffin Burrows|treasure]]. Answering incorrectly results in the Sphinx becoming infuriated, increasing its [[Health]] and [[Health|Maximum Health]] by 50%, as well as inflicting a stronger bleeding effect on players.
### Questions
The order of answers is randomized each time. In this article, they're presented in alphabetical order.
|Question|Answers|Correct Answer|
|-|-|-|
|How do you obtain the Dark Purple Dye?|Dark Auction Fishing The End|Dark Auction|
|How many floors are there in The Catacombs?|6 7 9|7|
|What does Junker Joel collect?|Dyes Junk Pests|Junk|
|What is the first type of slayer Maddox offers?|Enderman Spider Zombie|Zombie|
|What item do you use to kill Pests?|Axe Hoe Vacuum|Vacuum|
|What type of mob is exclusive to the Fishing Festival?|Shark Snake Whale|Shark|
|Where is the Titanoboa found?|Backwater Bayou Spider's Den The Park|Backwater Bayou|
|Where is Trevor the Trapper found?|Mushroom Desert The Hub The Park|Mushroom Desert|
|Which item rarity comes after Mythic?|Divine Legendary Rare|Divine|
|Which of these is NOT a pet?|Sheep Slime Slug|Slime|
|Which of these is NOT a type of Gemstone?|Amethyst Peridot Prismite|Prismite|
|Which type of Gemstone has the lowest Breaking Power?|Jade Ruby Topaz|Ruby|
|Who helps you apply Rod Parts?|Remi Roddy Rudy|Roddy|
|Who owns the Gold Essence Shop?|Gemma King Midas Marigold|Marigold|
|Who runs the Chocolate Factory?|Beppity Hipppity Hoppity|Hoppity|

### Loot Sharing
This section needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
Sphinx can also be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] or put in a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] to obtain [[Sphinx Shard]] and +500 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] (+5,000 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] when [[Charm Chance|Charmed]]) per shard. The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]]. Since this mob is [[Elusive]], [[Charm Chance|Charming]] is guaranteed to proc on kill.
## Game Messages
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Answering correctly|Right answer! The Sphinx is intrigued by your superior intellect.|
|Answering incorrectly|Wrong answer! Sphinx is infuriated by your inferior intellect!|

## Trivia
- The Sphinx is based on the Sphinx of Greek mythology, a creature that guarded the city of Thebes and killed travelers who could not answer its riddle. This is reflected in-game by the Sphinx quizzing the player on their SkyBlock knowledge.

- Sphinx with a Four Seasons Griffin Skin equipped Sphinx with a [[Four Seasons Griffin Skin]] equipped

# Storage
Categories: Article stubs, Pages with UIs, Storages
This article needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
[Storage]
Type: Interface
Appearance: Chest
The Storage is a section in the [[SkyBlock Menu]] and acts as a shortcut to the [[Ender Chest]], as well as a way to store and open [[Backpack|Backpacks]].
## Interface
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
## Usage
Clicking on the Storage icon in the [[SkyBlock Menu]]. will open the menu. Once inside, it will reveal the [[Player]]'s [[Ender Chest]] slots on the top, and a storage area for [[Backpack|Backpacks]] below.
There are 18 [[Backpack]] slots in total, however only slot #1 is unlocked by default. Slots 2-18 are unlocked through exchanging [[Fairy Souls]] at [[Tia the Fairy]].
[[Backpack|Backpacks]] cannot be removed from the Storage if they contain items. The Storage is also the only current way to open [[Backpack|Backpacks]].
You can unlock extra [[Ender Chest]] slots with [[SkyBlock Gems]].
## Trivia
- On the time of its release, the third [[Ender Chest]] section was removed, effectively deleting all items contained within it. It is not known if these items will be returned in any way.
- The storage can be quickly accessed with the command /storage or /st
- Backpacks that were stored in slots 2-18 before they were locked remained, it was only slots that were empty that were locked.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Apr 20, 2021|Added the Storage.|
|Sep 28, 2022|0.15|Locked [[Backpack]] slots 2-18.|

A Stranded Nymph is a [[Mythological|Mythological Creature]] that can be spawned from [[Griffin Burrows]] during the [[Mythological Ritual]].
## Spawning
A Stranded Nymph can be spawned by digging up [[Griffin Burrows]] with an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] or higher [[Griffin Pet]] equipped.
## Variants
|Level|Name|[[Griffin Pet]]; [[Rarity]]|Spawn Chance|Stats [[Health]]|Stats [[Damage]]|Drops Combat XP ([[Combat]])|Drops [[Experience]]|Drops [[Coins]]|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv44]|Blessed Stranded Nymph|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|22.22% (1 in 4.5 mobs)|20,000|100|66|48|14|
|[Lv83]|Stalwart Stranded Nymph|RARE ([[Rarity]])|16.67% (1 in 6 mobs)|125,000|250|124|91|27|
|[Lv128]|Venerable Stranded Nymph|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|14.29% (1 in 7 mobs)|400,000|750|192|140|42|
|[Lv178]|Exalted Stranded Nymph|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|13.91% (1 in 7.19 mobs)|1,250,000|1,750|267|195|59|
|[Lv208]|Empyrean Stranded Nymph|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|13.84% (1 in 7.22 mobs)|2,500,000|2,500|312|228|69|
## Behavior
A Stranded Nymph will move slowly towards the player. At 50% [[Health]], the Stranded Nymph will spawn a 5x5 grid of water that they move faster in.

Stranded Treecapitator is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Axes|Axe]]. It is a legacy version of the [[Treecapitator]], made exclusively for [[Stranded|☀ Stranded]] profiles to compensate for [[Sweep]] being disabled on [[Private Island|Private Islands]].

# The Great Spook
Aliases: Great Spook, Great Spook (event), Great Spook Event, Great spook event, The Great Spook Event
Categories: Article stubs, Events
This article needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
Great Spook 2026
:
Countdown:
Dates and times based on browser time.
The Great Spook is an annual [[Events|event]].
## Mechanics
Each Great Spook takes place in real-life October and lasts for about two weeks, with the exact start time varying. During the Great Spook, the amount of [[Spooky Festival|Spooky Festivals]] are usually increased and several exclusive NPCs and mobs spawn.
### Tyashoi Alchemist
During the event, the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] appears. Players who are at least [[Combat|Combat X (10)]] and have 10 hours of playtime can participate in their questline to obtain various exclusive items and the [[Eerie Pet]].
### Primal Fears
During the Great Spook, killing a mob has a small chance to spawn [[Primal Fear|Primal Fears]]. Once killed, they spawn a manifestation where, in order to be killed, a player must perform a specific action depending on the manifestation spawned. Once killed, they drop [[Dark Candy]].
### Vargul the Unearthed
[[Vargul the Unearthed]] appears in the [[Ruins]] zone during the Great Spook. Players can donate [[Dark Candy]] obtained from [[Primal Fear|Primal Fears]] to obtain various rewards.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Oct 25, 2021|Added Great Spook|
|Oct 25, 2021|Added [[Tyashoi Alchemist]].|
|Oct 9, 2024|Revamped [[Vargul the Unearthed]] shop.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Oct 28, 2021|Added Great Great Spook|
|Oct 28, 2021|Added [[Tyashoi Alchemist]].|
|Oct 24, 2023|0.19.6|Added [[Vargul the Unearthed]].|
|Oct 24, 2023|0.19.6|Added Primal Fears ([[Primal Fear]])|
|Oct 24, 2023|0.19.6|Added [[Dark Candy]].|
|Oct 15, 2024|0.20.7|Revamped [[Vargul the Unearthed]] shop.|

# The Mist
Categories: Article stubs, Locations, Dwarven Mines
This article needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
[The Mist]
Type: [[Combat]] location
Level: Mining XII (12) ([[Mining]])
Music: "Dwarven Mines ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Dwarven Mines]]
[Inhabitants]
Mobs ([[Mob]]): [[Ghost]]
[Resources]
Mob Drops: [[Coins]] [[Ghostly Boots]] [[Plasma]] [[Sorrow]] [[Volta]]
The Mist is a location in the [[Dwarven Mines]] located under [[Divan's Gateway]]. It houses [[Ghost|Ghosts]].
## Location
The Mist is made out of white stained glass, white wool, and ice. The Mist is filled with [[Ghost|Ghosts]], enemies that can be damaged, although their health is not displayed and is therefore difficult to find. They look like invisible charged creepers. They have 1M [[Health]] Health and deal high damage. It isn't recommended to go into The Mist in early game.
When the player enters the The Mist, the following message is displayed on their screen:
DANGER Powerful creatures reside in The Mist
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters The Mist, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
The Mist
■ Pain, only pain.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jan 15, 2021|0.11|Added The Mist.|

The Mountaintop is a [[Locations|Location]] in the [[Rift Dimension]]. It is based on the [[Mountain]].
## Features
The Mountaintop features many attractions. The player must have secured both the [[Vampiric Timecharm]] and have a minimum of 9:00 [[Rift Time]] remaining in order to access the location.
### The 7th Sin
One of [[Porhtal]]'s eyes, The 7th Sin, can be found on The Mountaintop. The eye's condition is ROGUE by default, and can be defeated to gain a permanent +30 [[Rift Time]] and the ability to instantly teleport to it from [[Porhtal]] or any of their other eyes. Teleporting to The 7th Sin costs 21m45s [[Rift Time]] and can only be done if the player has enough time remaining.
### Vending Machine
A Vending Machine can be found on The Mountaintop. Once a player pays 1,000 [[Motes]], it will dispense a potion with [[Gravity Potion|Gravity I]], which increases the chances of [[Puff|Puffs]] spawning.

- The /secretentrancetothemountaintop command returns Get goofed! when ran, but only works in the [[Rift Dimension]].
- Before release, the [[Celestial Timecharm]] was called the Mountain Timecharm.
- Before release, the Mountaintop had various false requirements to enter. Each requirement would be displayed for a few seconds before changing to a new one.

|(same as above)|Requires fishing for update to enter!|Refers to a joke in the Hypixel SkyBlock community, about fishing in various portals. The message Sorry, no update here :( was later added as feedback when fishing in any End Portal block.|

# Toughness
Categories: Article stubs, Confirmations needed
This article needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
⧉ Toughness is a measurement of how much [[Sweep]] is required to cut down a tree on [[Galatea]].
- Most trees on [[The Park]] have 0 ⧉ Toughness[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)].
- [[Fig Log|Fig Logs]] Trunks have 10 ⧉ Toughness, so a minimum of 10 [[Sweep]] is required to overcome the [[Sweep]] penalty.
- [[Mangrove Log|Mangrove Logs]] Trunks and Roots have 50 ⧉ Toughness, so a minimum of 50 [[Sweep]] is required to overcome the [[Sweep]] penalty.
- [[Helix Log|Helix Logs]] have 150 ⧉ Toughness, so a minimum of 150 [[Sweep]] is required to overcome the [[Sweep]] penalty.
- Branches have half the ⧉ Toughness of their other Tree Parts.
## Trivia
- [[Moonglade Marsh]]'s Chapter 7 hints at Trees of higher Toughness :
Truffle or Pinerise Trees require thousands of [[Sweep]]! But what are those?
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 5, 2025|Added Toughness.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 23, 2025|0.23|Added Toughness.|
|Aug 24, 2026|Changed [[Sweep]] formula to seemingly no longer use toughness.|

The Underground Lab is a [[Locations|Location]] in the [[Glowing Mushroom Cave]]. It can be entered by digging [[Coarse Dirt]] at 175 42 -470 after completing [[Beth]]'s quest.
## Overview
Upon accepting [[Beth]]'s visitor offers 4 times, the player can enter the Underground Lab where [[Beth]] will confront [[Jake]] for the first time. Once the dialogue ends, the player can claim a [[Jake's Plushie]].
After unlocking the [[Greenhouse]], [[Beth]] will visit again and the player can enter the Underground Lab a second time, revealing a secondary area where the [[Crop Analyzer]] can be accessed after more dialogue between [[Beth]] and [[Jake]].

# Coins
Aliases: Coin, Coins (Golden)
Categories: Bad currency number, More info needed, Article stubs, Currencies, Mechanics, Scam-Prone Items
[Coins]
Type: [[Currency]]
Amount: 1–9 Coins
Type: [[Currency]]
Amount: 10–99 Coins
Type: [[Currency]]
Amount: 100–999 Coins
Type: [[Currency]]
Amount: 1,000–1,999 Coins
Type: [[Currency]]
Amount: 2,000–4,999 Coins
Type: [[Currency]]
Amount: 5,000 Coins+
Coins are the main form of [[Currency]] in SkyBlock to buy and sell items. Unlike most things is possible to have fraction amounts of Coins, although they are typically not obtained in this way.
## Display
All [[Player|Players]] have their Coins displayed on the right side of the screen in the scoreboard. The Coin storage is displayed as a [[Purse]]. If the player has a [[Piggy Bank]], it is instead displayed as Piggy.
Player's Purse will show 1 digit of decimal amount of Coins held, if they are holding less than 10,000 Coins, otherwise the decimal value gets hidden. For example the Purse of 10,000.2 Coins will be displayed as 10,000 Coins. The Purse will also show that it's holding Coins (no decimal amount of coins), if their Purse is less than 1,000 Coins — so if the player earns 1 Coin when having 999 Coins, the display will change from 999 Coins to 1,000 Coins.
Within the player's Purse, only the first digit of decimal values is displayed. If there are 2 digits of decimal value, then the value gets rounded to the nearest even decimal. 0.25 Coins gets rounded to 0.2 Coins, 0.75 Coins gets rounded to 0.8 Coins and so on. Values of Coins lower than 0.1 Coins aren't displayed, but are still stored. Selling [[Stick]] to an NPC will give the player 0.25 Coins, but will only display 0.2 Coins, following that with a second [[Stick]] will then display 0.5 Coins accordingly.
## Storage
Any coins collected by the player goes into the player's [[Purse]]. Should the player not desire to hold some of these coins, they may be deposited in the [[Bank]], to a maximum of 50,000,000 Coins at the first level. There are seven levels and the final one can hold a max of 60,000,000,000 Coins (60B).
## Obtaining
There are various ways of obtaining Coins.
### Passive Methods
|Method|Description|
|-|-|
|Coin Allowance ([[Account & Profile Upgrades]])|Upgrading the Coins Allowance Profile Upgrade ([[Account & Profile Upgrades]]) will grant 10,000-50,000 Coins when logging in to SkyBlock per day|
|Bank Interest ([[Bank]])|Coins stored in the bank will accumulate Interest, which is granted every 3 SkyBlock Months ([[Time Systems]])|
|[[Budget Hopper]]|Minions with full inventory will start selling items for 50% their NPC Sell price|
|[[Enchanted Hopper]]|Minions with full inventory will start selling items for 70% their NPC Sell price|
|[[Talisman of Coins]]|Coins appear around the player occasionally on public islands|
|[[Ring of Coins]]|(same as above)|
|[[Artifact of Coins]]|(same as above)|
|[[Relic of Coins]]|(same as above)|
|[[Emerald Ring]]|Gain +5 Coins every minute|
|[[Emerald Artifact]]|Gain +20 Coins every minute|
### Active Methods
#### Killing Mobs
Killing [[Mob|Mobs]] reward a set number of coins based on the killed mob, there are various modifiers that increase the total amount per kill.
|Modifier|Type of Modifier|Bonus|Condition|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Bestiary]]||+2% Coins per bestiary level of killed mob||
|[[Scavenger Talisman]]; [[Scavenger Ring]]; [[Scavenger Artifact]]|Accessory ([[Accessories]])|+0.5–0.75 Coins per Mob Level ([[Mob]])||
|[[Mercenary Armor]]|[[Armor]]|+5 Coins|Combat VIII (8) ([[Combat]]), Mob Level ([[Mob]]) is at least 10|
|[[Eleanor's Armor]]|[[Armor]]|1.5x → 3x Coins|On Coins generated by the [[Scavenger]] Enchantment|
|Midas Touch ([[Attributes]]) ( [[Golden Ghoul Shard]])|Attribute ([[Attributes]])|+1–10 Coins||
|[[Scavenger]]|Enchantment ([[Enchantments]])|+0.3–1.8 Coins per Mob Level ([[Mob]])|Enchanting I (1) ([[Enchanting]])|
|[[Champion]]|Enchantment ([[Enchantments]])|+1.4–5 Coins|2nd hit to a mob|
|Bank ([[Bank (Enchantment)]])|Enchantment ([[Enchantments]])|+0.5–2.5 Coins|Enchanting XVI (16) ([[Enchanting]])|
|[[Golden Dragon Pet]]'s Symbiosis|Pet ([[Pets]]) perk|+5 Coins per other unique maxed Combat Pet ([[Pets]])|[Lv200] [[Golden Dragon Pet]]|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])+ [[Grandma Wolf Pet]]'s Kill Combo|Pet ([[Pets]]) perk|+2–10 Coins buff at 10 combo kills; and another +2-10 Coins buff at 30 combo kills|None, its perks are active even when not summoned.|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])+ [[Hound Pet]]'s Scavenger|Pet ([[Pets]]) perk|+100 Coins||
|[[Raider Sword]]|Sword ([[Swords]])|+20 Coins|Mob Level ([[Mob]]) is at least 10|
#### Selling
Selling items to a [[Shops|Merchant]] will exchange the selected item for a predetermined number of coins (certain items cannot be sold, such as minions). All NPCs will give the same amount coins for an item, and the amount of coins gained by selling cannot changed, the price of items when buying items however can be. If sold by accident, players can buy back the last 10 items sold to an NPC at any NPC's shop, for the same price they got when buying them. These up to 10 items disappear at 12 AM EST. [More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; What update added this)
Players can only generate 500,000,000 Coins every day, resetting at midnight GMT. Selling and buying back an item resets the coins that item generated, as the net amount of Coins generated from that is 0.
Putting items up for sale in the [[Auction House]] or at [[Bazaar Alley]] also gives the player Coins if another player buys them. These coins can be collected by talking to the [[Auction Master]] or [[Bazaar (NPC)|Bazaar]] or remotely via a [[Booster Cookie]].
#### Other Methods
|Method|Description|
|-|-|
|Skill ([[Skills]]) Leveling Rewards|Leveling up [[Skills]] rewards a set number of coins, with each level granting more coins up to 1,000,000 Coins|
|Player Trading ([[Trading]])|Players can Trade ([[Trading]]) Coins alongside other items|
|[[Fishing]]|Players can sometimes fish out a random amount of Coins from water or lava|
|[[Quests]]|Completing quests sometimes reward some Coins|
|[[Spooky Festival]]|Participating in the [[Spooky Festival]] will reward a set number of Coins based on the Bracket the player ends on at the end of the event.|
|[[Gifts]]|Opening gifts has a chance to reward a set number of Coins|
|[[Mythological Ritual]]|Digging up [[Griffin Burrows]] sometimes drops some Coins, the amount increases with higher rarity [[Griffin Pet]] equipped|
|[[Scorpius]]|When Scorpius gets elected, he rewards up to 1,000,000 Coins to all those who voted for him|
|[[Midas Jewel]]|The Gilded reforge has a rare chance to grant Coins to a nearby player upon killing an enemy|
|[[Golden Ball]]|The Bountiful reforge grants +0.2 Coins per broken crop|
|[[White Gift Talisman]]|Grants +5–25% Coins from [[Gifts]]|
|[[Green Gift Talisman]]|Grants +5–25% Coins from [[Gifts]]|
|[[Blue Gift Talisman]]|Grants +5–25% Coins from [[Gifts]]|
|[[Purple Gift Talisman]]|Grants +5–25% Coins from [[Gifts]]|
|[[Gold Gift Talisman]]|Grants +5–25% Coins from [[Gifts]]|
## Losing Coins
Upon death the player will lose half of the Coins in their [[Purse]], splitting Coins into decimals rather than rounding. If the player dies in certain ways, their purse will not be deducted. Coins are not removed from the purse if the player:
- Falls in the void on a [[Private Island]] or [[The Garden]], without being hit by a mob. (Note that other deaths, including Fall Damage, will still deduct Coins)
- Dies during the [[Trials of Fire]] (with one exception, see [[Trials of Fire|Trials of Fire#Bugs]])
- Dies from drowning on the islands of [[Galatea]]
- Dies in [[Dungeons]], [[Kuudra]], the [[Rift Dimension]], in [[Glacite Mineshafts]] (both to [[Cold]] and combat. Notably, dying in [[Glacite Tunnels]], regardless of the cause, will remove Coins.) and the [[Critter Safari|Safari Zone]]
[More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; Matriarch, Aranya; When this info is added, remove this.)
Dying in the previously mentioned also won't damage [[Piggy Bank]].
Reducing Coins loss on Death:
|Icon|Method|Reduction|Notes|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Phoenix Pet]]|VERY SPECIAL ([[Rarity]]) [[Phoenix Pet]]|100%||
|[[Booster Cookie]]|[[Booster Cookie]]|100%||
||Wolf Slayer Lvl VIII (8) ([[Wolf Slayer]])|5%|Stacks additively with other forms of Coins loss reduction.|
|[[Piggy Bank]]|[[Piggy Bank]]|100%|Only triggers when losing over 20,000 Coins (40,000 Coins or more in purse). Turns into a Cracked Piggy Bank ([[Piggy Bank]]) on death.|
|[[Piggy Bank]]|Cracked Piggy Bank ([[Piggy Bank]])|75%|Only triggers when losing over 20,000 Coins (40,000 Coins or more in purse). Turns into a Broken Piggy Bank ([[Piggy Bank]]) on death.|
|[[Bank (Enchantment)]]|Bank ([[Bank (Enchantment)]])|10-50%|Having 2 [[Armor]] pieces with Bank V ([[Bank (Enchantment)]]) will fully negate loss of Coins. Same applies for any other combination that results in the total level of the enchantments being 10 or higher.|
Players can also prevent losing their Coins by storing them in a [[Bank]].
Means that prevent death altogether will also prevent losing coins. This includes [[Spirit Mask]], [[Saving Grace]], [[Remnant of the Eye]] and [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] [[Phoenix Pet]] (as [[Rarity|VERY SPECIAL]] always prevents losing coins).
## Usage
### Markets and Trading
As the main currency of Hypixel SkyBlock, Coins can be exchanged for countless items through variety of means.
Players can use their Coins or other [[Currency|Currencies]] and sometimes Materials to purchase items from NPCs at [[Shops]], generally there is a limit of buying the same item 640 times per day from the same NPC. Certain items like most products sold by [[Builder]] have none. The daily limit for can be increased 10x by [[Diaz]]'s Shopping Spree perk. Items that can purchased from different shops, such as [[Iron Ingot]] from [[Mining Merchant|Mine Merchant]] and [[Iron Forger]] don't share stock, allowing players to purchase up to 1,280 (20 stacks) [[Iron Ingot]] per day. Despite this, [[Farm Merchant]] and [[Windmill Operator]] share their stock. The general rule of thumb is:
- Stackable items sold by NPC shops for Coins, that can be traded on [[Bazaar]], have a stock of 640 items.
  - Additionally, this also includes items that cannot be sold on Bazaar, but can be turned into items that can be: [[Block of Quartz]], [[Glowstone]], [[Sugar]] and [[Wet Sponge]].
  - However, the following items don't have a stock [[Netherrack]], [[Wool|White Wool]], [[Experience Bottle]], [[Superboom TNT]] and [[Common Foraging Fortune Booster]].
- Unstackable items sold by NPCs, items sold by NPCs for something other than Coins (including items sold for Coins and something else, like [[Chum Bucket|Empty Chum Bucket]]), or stackable items that cannot be sold on Bazaar don't have a stock.
- Items sold for [[Chocolate]], all items sold by [[Kiara]] and [[Sanger]], most items sold by [[Piggy]], [[Lukas the Aquarist]], [[Agnes]] and all of the following have their own unique stock: [[Enchanted Book]] ([[Divine Gift|Divine Gift I]]), [[The Art of Peace]], [[Fiesta Flask]], [[Packrat Shard]] and [[Freshly-Minted Coins]]
  - All of these are also not affected by [[Diaz]]'s Shopping Spree perk.
  - Certain shops contain perks which can only be purchased a specific amount of times in total on a profile.
Futhermore, players can use Coins to bid on items at the [[Auction House]]. If the player does not win a bid, and the auction ends, they may reclaim their Coins by talking to the [[Auction Master]]. The Coins will immediately be subtracted from the purse when the player make a bid. Players can also buy a BIN auction (Buy it now) to instantly buy an item without anyone being able to outbid the buyer.
Coins can also be used to buy items on [[Bazaar]], giving them the option to buy resources instantly, which will immediately deduct their Coins and give them the product if it is available, or place a buy order. Placing a buy order, similarly to placing a Big on the Auction House, will subtract Coins from the player's purse, but allows the players to get them back, if the order was not fully filled. If the order was partially filled, players will only be able to get unspent Coins.
Coins can also be used to trade directly with other players (for example in exchange for items or services), as well as Participating in a [[Dark Auction]], [[Richard|Stonks Auction]] or [[Shen's Auction]].
### Taxes and Fees
Certain mechanics have a tax or a fee which requires Coins or takes away a portion when claiming Coins. This includes starting or claiming an [[Auction House|Auction]], claiming Coins from [[Bazaar]] sell orders or [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]].
Certain items cost Coins to use, such as [[The Alchemist's Staff]], [[InfiniDirt™ Wand]] or potentially [[InfiniPot™]].
### NPC Services
This section needs more information. Please help expand it. Note: Likely missing some.
See more stubs.
Certain NPCs require Coins in exchange for various services.
- Starting [[Slayer|Slayer Quests]] at [[Maddox the Slayer]]
- Reforging items at [[Blacksmith]]
- Conjuring Rain on [[The Park]] from [[Vanessa]]
- Upgrading pets at [[Kat]]
- Levelling pets through [[Fann]]
- Removing applied [[Gemstone|Gemstones]] through [[Geo]]
- Removing applied [[Drill Parts]] through [[Jotraeline Greatforge]]
- Removing applied [[Rod Parts]] through [[Roddy]]
- Doing [[Treasure Hunter]]'s quest
- Analyzing [[Mutations]] at [[Jake]]
- Purchasing hints for hunting mobs from [[Teddy]] and [[Freddy]]
- Expanding player's [[Accessory Bag]] via [[Jacobus]]
- Increasing the bonus on [[Midas' Sword]] and [[Midas Staff]] through [[Zarina]]
- Buying [[Gwendolyn|Crystal Hollows Pass]] [[Gwendolyn]]
- Certain recipes at [[The Forge|Forge]] require Coins alongside other resources
- [[Bruuh]] gives the player access to a [[Fairy Souls|Fairy Soul]] for 1,000,000 Coins
- Extending the duration of [[Chocolate Rabbits|Chocobits]] by [[Time Systems|1 Year]]
Coins are requirements for certain [[Abiphones|Abiphone Contacts]]: [[Blacksmith]] requires 32,000,000 Coins, [[Jax]] can be paid 10,000,000 Coins as an alternative to doing [[Archery Range|Target Practice 4]], and [[Trevor]] requires 24 [[Pelts]] and 1,000,000 Coins for theirs.

- Currently unused emerald coin drop
## Trivia
- Coins, [[Motes]], [[Chocolate]], ✿ [[Sowdust]], certain [[Stats]] and [[Skills|Skill XP]] are the only values which the player can have fractional amounts of.

# Currency
Aliases: Currencies, Currency (disambiguation), Money
Categories: Bad currency number, More info needed, Currencies
[Currency]
Currency is, as the name suggests, a variety of in-game units of currency that are used for transactions. Currencies, unlike regular items, are not physical and cannot be interacted with outside of their shops.
Some currencies, like [[Coins]] and [[Essence]], are used for transactions between [[NPC|NPCs]], players, and other features; other currencies, such as ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]] or ✿ [[Sowdust]], are used to upgrade parts of the game.
## Coins
[[Coins]] are the primary unit of currency in SkyBlock. They are obtained through multiple sources and used to purchase items from [[NPC|NPCs]] alongside other sinks, such as the [[Dark Auction]] and [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]]: however, they are most commonly circulated through the [[Bazaar]] and [[Auction House]].
[[Coins]] can be kept in the [[Bank]] or [[Purse]]. Half of all [[Coins]] kept in the [[Purse]] are lost on death while not under the effect of a [[Piggy Bank]] or [[Booster Cookie]].
## Premium
### Bits
[[Bits]] are a form of currency primarily used to purchase items from the [[Bits Shop]] at [[Elizabeth]]. They can only be accumulated while under the effects of a [[Booster Cookie]]: the player's Bits Multiplier is increased by their [[Fame Ranks|Fame]] rank and [[Museum]] milestones.
[[Bits]] can also be spent on a variety of convenient, though rarely necessary, purchases, such as applying [[Dyes]] to [[Armor]]; specific [[City Projects|City Project]] contributions; purchasing additional [[Rift Dimension|Dimensional Infusions]]; or purchasing [[Maxwell|Tuning Point]] templates at [[Maxwell]].
### Gems
[[SkyBlock Gems]] are a premium currency, primarily used for purchase of the [[Booster Cookie]]. They can only be purchased from the Hypixel Store: using a creator code provides a 5% discount on purchase, and 6[[Ranks|[MVP++]]] players gain 500 (7 stacks plus 52) [[SkyBlock Gems]] per month. Prices are as follows:
|Quantity|Base Price|Creator Code|
|-|-|-|
|700x|$5.99 USD|$5.69 USD|
|1800x|$14.99 USD|$14.24 USD|
|3750x|$29.99 USD|$28.49 USD|
|7600x|$59.99 USD|$56.99 USD|
|17000x|$119.99 USD|$113.99 USD|
[[SkyBlock Gems]] are also used to purchase [[Account & Profile Upgrades]] and [[Fire Sale|Fire Sales]] at [[Elizabeth]], cosmetics at [[Taylor]], and [[Barn Skins]] at [[The Garden|SkyMart]].
## Essence
[[Essence]] is a variety of different currencies. It can be spent at [[Essence Shops]] or used to upgrade items by adding [[Star Upgrades]].
There are 10 types of Essence: [[Wither Essence|Wither]], [[Undead Essence|Undead]], [[Dragon Essence|Dragon]], [[Spider Essence|Spider]], [[Gold Essence|Gold]], [[Diamond Essence|Diamond]], [[Ice Essence|Ice]], [[Crimson Essence|Crimson]], [[Forest Essence|Forest]], [[Safari Essence|Safari]] and [[Fossil Essence|Fossil]]. Additionally, [[Sun Gecko Essence]] is accessible only in the [[Rift Dimension]].
|Essence|Essence Shop|
|-|-|
|[[Wither Essence]]|[[Malik]] in the [[Catacombs Entrance]] and [[Dungeon Hub]]|
|[[Undead Essence]]|(same as above)|
|[[Crimson Essence]]|[[Vulcan]] in the [[Forgotten Skull]] and [[Dungeon Hub]]|
|[[Spider Essence]]|[[Spider Tamer]] in [[Arachne's Sanctuary]]|
|[[Dragon Essence]]|[[Tyzzo]] in the [[Void Slate]]|
|[[Gold Essence]]|[[Marigold]] in the [[Royal Mines]]|
|[[Diamond Essence]]|[[Gemma]] in the [[Crystal Nucleus]]|
|[[Ice Essence]]|[[Frozen Alex]] by the [[Jerry Pond]]|
|[[Forest Essence]]|[[Rosemary]] in the [[Murkwater Outpost]]|
|[[Safari Essence]]|[[Archie]] in the [[Critter Safari Entrance]] and [[Critter Safari]]|
|[[Fossil Essence]]|[[Researcher Beryl]] in the [[Fossil Research Center]]|
|[[Sun Gecko Essence]]|[[Oruo The Almighty]] in the [[Continuum]]|
## Powder
There are 3 varieties of Powder: ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]], ᠅ [[Gemstone Powder]], and ᠅ [[Glacite Powder]]. They are used to level up [[Heart of the Mountain]] perks.
- ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]] is used to level up [[Heart of the Mountain|HotM]] perks accessible between Tiers 1-3;
- ᠅ [[Gemstone Powder]] is used to level up [[Heart of the Mountain|HotM]] perks accessible between Tiers 4-7;
- ᠅ [[Glacite Powder]] is used to level up [[Heart of the Mountain|HotM]] perks accessible between Tiers 8-10.
Additionally, they can be used to level up the [[Heart of the Mountain|Core of the Mountain]], unlocked at [[Heart of the Mountain|HotM V (5)]]; ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]] is used to unlock Tiers 2-3, ᠅ [[Gemstone Powder]] is used to unlock Tiers 4-7, and ᠅ [[Glacite Powder]] is used to unlock Tiers 8-10.
### Mithril Powder
᠅ [[Mithril Powder]] is primarily obtained from mining [[Mithril]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]], [[Crystal Hollows]], and [[Glacite Tunnels]]. It is also obtained through [[Commissions]], [[Dwarven Mines|Mining Events]], killing certain mobs, and completing certain events.
It is used to level up:
- Tier I Perks:
  - Mining Speed: Grants increased [[Mining Speed]].
- Tier II Perks:
  - Mining Fortune: Grants increased [[Mining Fortune]].
  - Titanium Insanium: Increases the chances of converting a mined [[Mithril]] block to [[Titanium]].
- Tier III Perks:
  - Efficient Miner: Grants increased [[Mining Spread]].
  - Quick Forge: Reduces [[The Forge|Forge]] times.
### Gemstone Powder
᠅ [[Gemstone Powder]] is primarily obtained from mining [[Gemstone|Gemstones]] in the [[Crystal Hollows]] and [[Glacite Tunnels]]. It is also obtained through [[Commissions]], [[Treasure Chest|Treasure Chests]], and completing the [[Crystal Nucleus]].
It is used to level up:
- Tier IV Perks:
  - Old School: Grants increased [[Ore Fortune]].
  - Professional: Grants increased [[Mining Speed]] on [[Gemstone|Gemstones]].
  - Mole: Grants increased [[Mining Spread]] on [[Hard Stone]].
  - Gem Lover: Grants increased [[Gemstone Fortune]].
  - Seasoned Mineman: Grants increased [[Mining Wisdom]].
- Tier VI Perks:
  - Blockhead: Grants increased [[Block Fortune]].
  - Subterranean Fisher: Grants increased [[Fishing Speed]] and [[Sea Creature Chance]] on [[Mining Islands]].
  - Keep It Cool: Grants increased [[Heat Resistance]].
  - Lonesome Miner: Grants increased [[Strength]], [[Crit Chance]], [[Crit Damage]], [[Health]], and [[Defense]] on [[Mining Islands]].
  - Great Explorer: Grants increased chances to find [[Treasure Chest|Treasure Chests]] in the [[Crystal Hollows]] and reduces the number of locks on them.
- Tier VII Perks:
  - Speedy Mineman: Grants increased [[Mining Speed]].
  - Powder Buff: Increases all Powder gain from all sources.
  - Fortunate Mineman: Grants increased [[Mining Fortune]].
### Glacite Powder
᠅ [[Glacite Powder]] is primarily obtained from mining [[Glacite]] in the [[Glacite Tunnels]]. It is also obtained through [[Commissions]], opening [[Frozen Corpses]], using the [[Fossil Excavator]], killing [[Glacite Maniacs]] and [[Littlefoot]], and other sources.
It is used to level up:
- Tier VIII Perks:
  - No Stone Unturned: Grants increased chances to find [[Suspicious Scrap|Suspicious Scraps]].
  - Strong Arm: Grants increased [[Mining Speed]] on [[Dwarven Metals]].
  - Steady Hand: Grants increased [[Gemstone Spread]] in [[Glacite Mineshafts]]
  - Warm Hearted: Grants increased [[Cold Resistance]].
  - Surveyor: Grants increased chances to find [[Glacite Mineshafts]].
- Tier IX Perks:
  - Metal Head: Grants increased [[Dwarven Metal Fortune]].
  - Rags to Riches: Grants increased [[Mining Fortune]] in [[Glacite Mineshafts]].
  - Eager Adventurer: Grants increased [[Mining Speed]] in [[Glacite Mineshafts]].
- Tier X Perks:
  - Crystalline: Increases the chances of finding [[Glacite Mineshafts]] containing [[Gemstone Crystals]].
  - Gifts from the Departed: Grants increased chances of gaining +1 item from looting [[Frozen Corpses]].
  - Mining Master: Grants increased [[Pristine]].
  - Dead Man's Chest: Grants increased chances of spawning 1 additional [[Frozen Corpses|Frozen Corpse]].
  - Vanguard Seeker: Increases the chances of finding the Fairy [[Glacite Mineshafts|Glacite Mineshaft]], which contains a [[Frozen Corpses|Vanguard Corpse]].
## Whispers
There are 2 varieties of Whispers: [[Forest Whispers]], and [[Desert Whispers]]. They are used to level up [[Heart of the Forest]] perks.
- [[Forest Whispers]] is used to level up [[Heart of the Forest|HotF]] perks accessible between Tiers 1-3;
- [[Desert Whispers]] is used to level up [[Heart of the Forest|HotF]] perks accessible between Tiers 4-8;
Additionally, they can be used to level up the [[Heart of the Forest|Center of the Forest]], unlocked at [[Heart of the Forest|HotF V (5)]]; [[Forest Whispers]] is used to unlock Tiers 2-3, and [[Desert Whispers]] is used to unlock Tiers 4-5.
### Forest Whispers
[[Forest Whispers]] can be obtained by doing the following in [[Moonglade Marsh]].
|Source|Description|
|-|-|
|Cutting Trees|1 Forest Whisper ([[Forest Whispers]]) per log cut|
|[[Tree Gifts]]|10 [[Forest Whispers]] per Tree Gift ([[Tree Gifts]])|
|Berries|50 [[Forest Whispers]] per [[Lushlilac]] bush harvested|
|Agatha's Contest ([[Starlyn Contest]])|[[Forest Whispers]] for reaching certain brackets|
### Desert Whispers
[[Desert Whispers]] can be obtained by doing the following in [[Torrhus Canyon]].
|Source|Description|
|-|-|
|Cutting Trees|[More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.) per log cut|
|[[Tree Gifts]]|[More Info Needed] per Tree Gift ([[Tree Gifts]])|
|Floor Drops|250 [[Desert Whispers]]|
|[[Ruby Veilshroom]]|[More Info Needed] per [[Ruby Veilshroom]] harvested|
|Miria's Contest ([[Starlyn Contest]])|[More Info Needed] for reaching certain brackets|
## Copper
[[Copper]] is the primary currency in [[The Garden]]. There are 2 main ways of obtaining [[Copper]]:
- Accepting [[The Garden|Garden Visitor]] offers while on [[The Garden]];
- Analyzing [[Mutations]] with the [[Crop Analyzer]] in the [[Underground Lab]].
[[Copper]] is used to purchase various items from [[The Garden|The Desk]]. This includes:
- Items in [[The Garden|SkyMart]], including:
  - farming essentials, such as the [[Builder's Ruler]], sets of [[Equipment]], and more
  - specialized Mk. I [[Farming Tools]], such as the [[Euclid's Wheat Sickle|Euclid's Wheat Sickle Mk. I]] and [[Melon Dicer|Melon Dicer Mk. I]]
  - items pertaining to [[Pests]], such as [[Vacuums]] and the [[Sprayonator]].
- [[Copper|Crop Upgrades]], which increases the player's [[Farming Fortune]] for specific crops;
- [[Composter|Composter Upgrades]], which increases the [[Composter]]'s Speed, Multi Drop Chance, [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] Cap, Organic Matter Cap, and Cost Reduction;
- [[Copper|Greenhouse Upgrades]], which upgrades the [[Greenhouse]]'s Growth Speed, Plant Yield, and Plot Limit.
## Sowdust
✿ [[Sowdust]] is a currency obtained from harvesting crops in [[The Garden]] using a [[Farming Tools|Specialized Farming Tool]] and harvesting crops in the [[Greenhouse]]. It is used to level up [[Garden Chips]], which provide permanent buffs to Farming.

## Medals
Participating in [[Jacob's Farming Contest]] rewards the player with [[Medals]]. There are 3 varieties of Medals: [[Medals|Bronze]], [[Medals|Silver]], and [[Medals|Gold]]. These can be spent at [[Anita]] for special items.
|Bracket|Medal|Top % Required|
|-|-|-|
|Bronze|Bronze Medal ([[Medals]])|60%|
|Silver|Silver Medal ([[Medals]])|30%|
|Gold|Gold Medal ([[Medals]])|10%|
|Platinum|Gold Medal ([[Medals]]); Bronze Medal ([[Medals]])|5%|
|Diamond|Gold Medal ([[Medals]]); Silver Medal ([[Medals]])|2%|
Additionally, a [[Medals|Silver Medal]] can be exchanged for 2 [[Medals|Bronze Medal]], and a [[Medals|Gold Medal]] can be exchanged for 4 [[Medals|Silver Medal]], and vice versa. They can also be exchanged for [[Jacob's Ticket|Jacob's Tickets]].
## Harvest Feast
### Seasoning
[[Seasoning]] is a currency exclusive to the [[Harvest Feast]] and [[Harvest Feast|Grand Feast]]. [[Seasoning]] is obtained from harvesting in-season crops: it is a [[Rare Crops|Rare Crop]], and as such is affected by [[Overbloom]].
[[Seasoning]] is used to unlock [[Feast Flask|Feast Flasks]] from [[Harvest Feast|Feast Milestones]] at [[Feast Chef Ted]].
### Kernels
[[Kernels]] are a currency exclusive to the [[Harvest Feast|Grand Feast]]. [[Kernels]] are obtained from donating [[Seasoning]] and from reaching [[Harvest Feast|Grand Feast Milestones]].
[[Kernels]] are used to purchase items from [[Feast Baker Scott]].
## Pelts
[[Pelts]] are a currency that are used to purchase Tier XII [[Farming]] [[Minions]] at [[Tony]], alongside other items at [[Talbot]]. They are obtained from killing Trapper Mobs, which are summoned from starting Trapper Quests at [[Trevor]]. The amount of [[Pelts]] obtained from Trapper Mobs are as follows:
|Rarity|Pelts Default|Pelts Max|
|-|-|-|
|Trackable|2|6|
|Untrackable|4|9|
|Undetected|6|12|
|Endangered|8|15|
|Elusive|16|27|
Multiple items increase the amount of [[Pelts]] a player obtains at once:
- [[Jake's Plushie]]: +1
- [[Trapper Crest]]: +1.1-1.5x
- [[Pelt Belt]]: +1
## Trivia
- There is a 2 billion limit on some [which? more info needed] currencies. This was added in a shadow patch after 2026-04-17 to prevent integer overflow.

# Purse
Categories: Bad currency number, More info needed, Mechanics
The Purse refers to the amount of [[Coins]] players have on them, to spend them at any time. Players can alternatively store their [[Coins]] in a [[Bank]]. If the player owns a [[Piggy Bank]], their purse will be displayed as "Piggy" instead.
## Display
Player's Purse will show 1 digit of decimal amount of [[Coins]] held, if they are holding less than 10,000 [[Coins]], otherwise the decimal value gets hidden. For example the Purse of 10,000.2 [[Coins]] will be displayed as 10,000 [[Coins]]. The Purse will also show that it's holding [[Coins]] (no decimal amount of coins), if their Purse is less than 1,000 [[Coins]] — so if the player earns 1 [[Coins|Coin]] when having 999 [[Coins]], the display will change from 999 [[Coins]] to 1,000 [[Coins]].
Within the player's Purse, only the first digit of decimal values is displayed. If there are 2 digits of decimal value, then the value gets rounded to the nearest even decimal. 0.25 [[Coins]] gets rounded to 0.2 [[Coins]], 0.75 [[Coins]] gets rounded to 0.8 [[Coins]] and so on. Values of [[Coins]] lower than 0.1 [[Coins]] aren't displayed, but are still stored. Selling [[Stick]] to an NPC will give the player 0.25 [[Coins]], but will only display 0.2 [[Coins]], following that with a second [[Stick]] will then display 0.5 [[Coins]] accordingly.
## Losing Coins
Upon death the player will lose half of the [[Coins]] in their Purse, splitting [[Coins]] into decimals rather than rounding. If the player dies in certain ways, their purse will not be deducted. [[Coins]] are not removed from the purse if the player:
- Falls in the void on a [[Private Island]] or [[The Garden]], without being hit by a mob. (Note that other deaths, including Fall Damage, will still deduct [[Coins]])
- Dies during the [[Trials of Fire]] (with one exception, see [[Trials of Fire|Trials of Fire#Bugs]])
- Dies from drowning on the islands of [[Galatea]]
- Dies in [[Dungeons]], [[Kuudra]], the [[Rift Dimension]], in [[Glacite Mineshafts]] (both to [[Cold]] and combat. Notably, dying in [[Glacite Tunnels]], regardless of the cause, will remove [[Coins]].) and the [[Critter Safari|Safari Zone]]
[More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; Matriarch, Aranya; When this info is added, remove this.)
Dying in the previously mentioned also won't damage [[Piggy Bank]].
Reducing [[Coins]] loss on Death:
|Icon|Method|Reduction|Notes|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Phoenix Pet]]|VERY SPECIAL ([[Rarity]]) [[Phoenix Pet]]|100%||
|[[Booster Cookie]]|[[Booster Cookie]]|100%||
||Wolf Slayer Lvl VIII (8) ([[Wolf Slayer]])|5%|Stacks additively with other forms of [[Coins]] loss reduction.|
|[[Piggy Bank]]|[[Piggy Bank]]|100%|Only triggers when losing over 20,000 [[Coins]] (40,000 [[Coins]] or more in purse). Turns into a Cracked Piggy Bank ([[Piggy Bank]]) on death.|
|[[Piggy Bank]]|Cracked Piggy Bank ([[Piggy Bank]])|75%|Only triggers when losing over 20,000 [[Coins]] (40,000 [[Coins]] or more in purse). Turns into a Broken Piggy Bank ([[Piggy Bank]]) on death.|
|[[Bank (Enchantment)]]|Bank ([[Bank (Enchantment)]])|10-50%|Having 2 [[Armor]] pieces with Bank V ([[Bank (Enchantment)]]) will fully negate loss of [[Coins]]. Same applies for any other combination that results in the total level of the enchantments being 10 or higher.|
Players can also prevent losing their [[Coins]] by storing them in a [[Bank]].
Means that prevent death altogether will also prevent losing coins. This includes [[Spirit Mask]], [[Saving Grace]], [[Remnant of the Eye]] and [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] [[Phoenix Pet]] (as [[Rarity|VERY SPECIAL]] always prevents losing coins).
## Trivia
- Before April 12, 2021, when [[Dante]] was elected, players wouldn't lose their [[Coins]] upon death. This turned out to be a glitch and got patched.
## History

|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Purse.|
|Mar 31, 2021|Players don't lose [[Coins]] on death, due to the presence of [[Dante]].|
|Apr 12, 2021|Players now lose [[Coins]] on death.|

# Purse/Display
Categories: Bad currency number
Player's Purse will show 1 digit of decimal amount of [[Coins]] held, if they are holding less than 10,000 [[Coins]], otherwise the decimal value gets hidden. For example the Purse of 10,000.2 [[Coins]] will be displayed as 10,000 [[Coins]]. The Purse will also show that it's holding [[Coins]] (no decimal amount of coins), if their Purse is less than 1,000 [[Coins]] — so if the player earns 1 [[Coins|Coin]] when having 999 [[Coins]], the display will change from 999 [[Coins]] to 1,000 [[Coins]].
Within the player's Purse, only the first digit of decimal values is displayed. If there are 2 digits of decimal value, then the value gets rounded to the nearest even decimal. 0.25 [[Coins]] gets rounded to 0.2 [[Coins]], 0.75 [[Coins]] gets rounded to 0.8 [[Coins]] and so on. Values of [[Coins]] lower than 0.1 [[Coins]] aren't displayed, but are still stored. Selling [[Stick]] to an NPC will give the player 0.25 [[Coins]], but will only display 0.2 [[Coins]], following that with a second [[Stick]] will then display 0.5 [[Coins]] accordingly.

# Enrichments
Aliases: Attack Speed Enrichment, Critical Chance Enrichment, Critical Damage Enrichment, Defence Enrichment, Defense Enrichment, Enrichment, Ferocity Enrichment, Health Enrichment, Intelligence Enrichment, Magic Find Enrichment, Sea Creature Chance Enrichment, Speed Enrichment, Strength Enrichment
Categories: Bits Shop, Special items
Enrichments are a category of [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] rarity items that give bonuses to [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]], [[Rarity|MYTHIC]], [[Rarity|SPECIAL]], and [[Rarity|VERY SPECIAL]] rarity [[Accessories]]. There are a variety of them, ranging from [[Health]] to [[Magic Find]]. They can be bought from [[Elizabeth]] for 5,000 [[Bits]] each, and they are consumed on use.
A player can use an [[Accessory Enrichment Swapper]] to change the enrichment of all enriched accessories in their accessory bag, which saves on buying several different enrichments for one accessory. It is also recommended to always buy the cheapest enriched accessory at the [[Auction House]] before changing its enrichment using the Accessory Enrichment Swapper to whatever enrichment the player needs, as that will almost always save money.
As of 0.27.2, the maximum number of enrichments is 68.

|[[Enrichments]]|Intelligence Enrichment|SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])|Enrich an accessory with the power of +2 [[Intelligence]].|5,000|
|[[Enrichments]]|Critical Damage Enrichment|SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])|Enrich an accessory with the power of +1 [[Crit Damage]].|5,000|
|[[Enrichments]]|Critical Chance Enrichment|SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])|Enrich an accessory with the power of +1 [[Crit Chance]].|5,000|
|[[Enrichments]]|Strength Enrichment|SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])|Enrich an accessory with the power of +1 [[Strength]].|5,000|
|[[Enrichments]]|Defense Enrichment|SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])|Enrich an accessory with the power of +1 [[Defense]].|5,000|

## Trivia
- A warning will appear when an enrichment would apply to an already enriched accessory, even if there is no enrichment on the accessory.
- Despite the description only mentioning [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] and [[Rarity|MYTHIC]], enrichments can be applied on [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] and [[Rarity|VERY SPECIAL]] accessories (currently only [[Hatcessories]]), and presumably on [[Rarity|DIVINE]] ones as well if they can be obtained outside of the [[Rift Dimension]].

# Ender Nodes
Aliases: End Node, End Nodes, End node, End nodes, Ender Node, Glowing Block, Glowing Blocks, Magic Ore, Node, Shiny Block, Shiny Blocks
Categories: Blocks, The End, Ender
[Ender Nodes]
An Ender Node
Ender Nodes, also known as Shiny Blocks or Glowing Blocks, are blocks found in [[The End]]. They have the appearance of [[Hardened Clay|Purple Hardened Clay]] and emit purple particles. Upon breaking, loot will drop.
## Mechanics
An Ender Node is a [[Hardened Clay|Purple Hardened Clay]] block that replaces [[End Stone]] or [[Obsidian]]. Mining an Ender Node will cause it to drop items or spawn [[Nest Endermite|Nest Endermites]]. Afterwards, it first turns into [[Bedrock]] then and 5 seconds after into [[End Stone]] or [[Obsidian]].
Initially, 18 [[End Stone]] and 18 [[Obsidian]] blocks are replaced with their respective Ender Nodes around the island. Every 0.25 seconds, if there's less than 18 Ender Nodes of a respective type, another one spawns at a random spot.
### Drops
While the block is replaced with the [[Hardened Clay|Purple Hardened Clay]], initial one ( [[End Stone]] or [[Obsidian]]) is still used to determine the drop. [[Stonk]] nullifies the [[Nest Endermite|Nest Endermites]] outcome.
Following items can obtained from Ender Nodes:

Amount of [[Nest Endermite|Nest Endermites]] spawned can be increased with [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Endermite Pet]] and [[Attributes|Mite Spawner]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Trivia
- Breaking an Ender Node for the first time will reward the player with the [[Achievements|"Oh Shiny" Achievement]].
- Ender Nodes have been referred to as different things over time:
  - In 0.7.11 changelog, they were referred to as nodes.
  - They were called "Glowing Block" in the [[Achievements|"Oh Shiny" Achievement]]'s description "Mine a Glowing Block on the End Island".
  - Multiple players refered to them as "Shiny Blocks".
  - Finally, they were named Ender Nodes since October 13, 2022 on the [[Alpha Hypixel Network]].

# Arrow Poison
Categories: Bows, Uncommon items
Arrow Poisons are consumables which grant special effects to fired arrows. They are [[Drop Chance|3Occasional]] drops from Tier II and above [[Tarantula Broodfather]] ( [[Toxic Arrow Poison]]) and [[Voidgloom Seraph]] ( [[Twilight Arrow Poison]]).
## Usage
Arrow Poisons stored in the Inventory or [[Quiver]] are automatically consumed when firing an arrow, applying their effects to all mobs that are hit. Arrow Poisons stored in the [[Slayer Sack]] will not be consumed.
Arrow Poisons are not affected by [[Infinite Quiver]] or the [[Skeleton Master Armor|Skeleton Master Chestplate]]'s passive ability, and they do not affect the [[Bonemerang]] or the [[Terminator]]'s Salvation ability.
## List
|Slot|Name|Effect|Obtaining|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Toxic Arrow Poison]]|[[Toxic Arrow Poison]]|Arrows deal an additional +10% of damage as poison and reduce healing by 24% over 4 seconds.|[[Spider Slayer]]|
|[[Twilight Arrow Poison]]|[[Twilight Arrow Poison]]|Arrows apply a ᛤ poison that increases all incoming damage by 10% for 20s.|[[Enderman Slayer]]|

# Shortbows
Aliases: Short Bow, Shortbow
Categories: Bows
[Shortbows]
Shortbows are a type of [[Bows|Bow]] that shoot instantly. They can be upgraded in the same way as [[Bows]] can.

This article is a community resource. This page has been provided as a helpful resource for Hypixel SkyBlock players.
Note: The guild leaderboards that are frequently referred to are not official.
Guilds are a huge part of the Hypixel Network. A lot of guilds tend to focus on Hypixel SkyBlock instead of other games. Joining such a guild is beneficial due to easier advertising, dungeon/dragon parties and more. Here is a list of quite a few guilds, along with their requirements and a bunch of other details.
## Guilds
Guidelines:
This list is open to community members. Any players can add their guild to the list if the guild is community-friendly. Please note the following guidelines:
- Please add new guilds at the bottom of the table.
- You may help by adding missing info to existing entries.
- Do not remove other guilds unless they got disbanded or something similar.
- If you have valid reasons to otherwise believe that a guild should not be added, please discuss on the talk page.
- All entries may be subject to additional review by the staff team at any time.
Disclaimer:
This page contains information that is provided by the community. The wiki does not sponsor, nor endorse any guild or organizations that are listed below. This page is not an article. This page is intended for self promotion. There is not a criterion for the guilds to attain before adding themselves to the list, nor the list of famous people.

# Pet Candies
Aliases: Pet Candy, Pet Food
Categories: Carrot Collection, Pets
Pet Food are items that can be given to [[Pets]] to grant it XP, without gaining its corresponding [[Skills|Skill]] experience.
A [[Pets|Pet]] may be fed up to 10 Pet Candy during its lifetime. Each candy fed will count as 1 use, regardless of the type of candy.
Other types of Pet Food that aren't Carrot Candy can be applied infinitely. These are [[Pet Cake]], [[Chyme]], [[Gabagool (disambiguation)|Gabagool]], and [[Fish Food]].
## Usage
To use Pet Food, right-click the pet while holding the food.
A [[Caducous Feeder]] may also be used to feed a pet [[Ultimate Carrot Candy]]. This will not add to its candy counter, and can be used even if the pet already has the maximum of 10 candies used. It also grants an extra 10% XP on top of the normal amount the pet would earn from an [[Ultimate Carrot Candy]]. However, it only has two charges, resetting after 20 hours.
## Types of Pet Candy
Feeding any type of candy to a pet grants it pet XP, counting towards its total XP levels.
|Name|Pet XP|Craft/Source|Pet|[[Coins]] per pet XP|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Simple Carrot Candy]]|20,000|Recipe: [[Carrot]] x64 ×9 → [[Simple Carrot Candy]]|Any||
|[[Great Carrot Candy]]|200,000|Recipe: [[Enchanted Carrot]] x40 ×8 + [[Simple Carrot Candy]] → [[Great Carrot Candy]]|Any||
|[[Superb Carrot Candy]]|750,000|Recipe: [[Enchanted Golden Carrot]] x3 ×8 + [[Great Carrot Candy]] → [[Superb Carrot Candy]]|Any||
|[[Ultimate Carrot Candy]]|1,500,000|Recipe: [[Superb Carrot Candy]] ×8 + [[Ultimate Carrot Candy Upgrade]] → [[Ultimate Carrot Candy]] x10|Any|or with; [[Caducous Feeder]]|
|[[Pet Cake]]|10,000|[[Oringo]] ([[The Garden]])|Any||
|[[Chyme]]|90,000|[[Aranya]]|Any||
|[[Fish Food]]|Varies|[[Loudmouth Bass]]|Any||
|[[Crude Gabagool]]|100|[[Inferno Minion]]|[[Wisp Pet]]||
|[[Fuel Gabagool]]|3,200|Recipe: [[Crude Gabagool]] x3/[[Sulphuric Coal]] ×8 + [[Sulphuric Coal]]/[[Very Crude Gabagool]] → [[Fuel Gabagool]]/[[Fuel Gabagool]] x8|[[Wisp Pet]]||
|[[Very Crude Gabagool]]|19,200|Recipe: [[Crude Gabagool]] x24 ×8 → [[Very Crude Gabagool]]|[[Wisp Pet]]||
|[[Heavy Gabagool]]|102,400|Recipe: [[Fuel Gabagool]] x3 ×8 + [[Sulphuric Coal]] → [[Heavy Gabagool]]|[[Wisp Pet]]||
|[[Hypergolic Gabagool]]|3,276,800|Recipe: [[Heavy Gabagool]] x3 ×4 + [[Sulphuric Coal]] → [[Hypergolic Gabagool]]|[[Wisp Pet]]||
## Trivia
- Having candy used showing on a pet usually makes players consider it lower value, especially if it's not max level and its candy uses have been wasted with lower quality candies.
- If a pet with [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] or higher rarity has gained enough XP to be max level even without the XP from the candies, the number of candies used will disappear. However, it is still exposed in the item's NBT data, and may be shown by mods or third-party websites such as SkyCrypt.
- Feeding a pet non-candy food, or candies with the [[Caducous Feeder]], will not have a visible effect on the pet, even in its NBT data, and is therefore practically indistinguishable from leveling it naturally.
- Gabagool is affected by Pet XP boosts, such as [[Beastmaster Crest]], [[Diana]], [[All Skills Exp Boost]] and [[Taming]], when being fed to [[Wisp Pet|Wisp Pets]]. Other Pet Foods however are not.

# Dungeon Reward Chest
Aliases: Bedrock Chest, Diamond Chest, Dungeon Chest, Dungeon Loot Chest, Dungeon Loot Chests, Dungeon Post-Boss Chest, Emerald Chest, Gold Chest, Obsidian Chest, Post-Boss Chest, Wooden Chest
Categories: Catacombs, Dungeons, Pages with math equations
[Dungeon Reward Chest]
A render of a bedrock loot chest, the highest tier possible.
Dungeon Reward Chests are loot chests that appear at the end of runs in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]]. They are the source of rewards for defeating [[Bosses]] in [[Dungeons]].
## Overview
At the end of a Dungeon run, several chests will appear. Three chests will always spawn, with up to three additional chests spawning if players perform exceptionally. Players cannot open chests if they do not reach their 2nd [[Class Milestones|Class Milestone]].
Money will be taken from the [[Purse]] first before taking money from the [[Bank]]. If there is not enough money in the Purse, then players in a [[Co-op]] will have their Dungeon chests take away money from their [[Bank|Personal Bank]] account first. However, if a player does not have enough money in their personal bank, then the chest will take money from the co-op bank instead.
Only one chest can be claimed per [[Catacombs]] run, unless a [[Dungeon Chest Key]] is used, which allows two to be claimed. Chests can be re-rolled once using a [[Kismet Feather]].
Unclaimed chests can be accessed by speaking to [[Croesus]] in the [[Dungeon Hub]]. These only last for 72 hours, and there is a cap of 60 runs.
## Chest Types
Chests do not appear in [[The Catacombs - Entrance]]. Unlike the Wooden, Gold, and Diamond chests, the additional chests have [[Dungeon Score]] requirements. Chest prices vary depending on the floor and the items the chests contain.
The Bedrock chest is only obtainable in [[The Catacombs - Floor V|Floor V]], [[The Catacombs - Floor VI|Floor VI]], and [[The Catacombs - Floor VII|Floor VII]].
In [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]] while in [[Catacombs|Master Mode]], completing the entire run will grant every type of chest, regardless of score. Similarly, failing a run but still reaching [[The Wither King]] will grant every type of chest, except the Bedrock Chest, regardless of score.
|Chest Rank|Requirements|Base Cost Floor I|Base Cost Floor II|Base Cost Floors III-IV|Base Cost Floors V-VII|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Wooden Chest|Defeat Boss; Pass Class Milestone ([[Class Milestones]]) 2|Free|Free|Free|Free|
|Gold Chest|Defeat Boss; Pass Class Milestone ([[Class Milestones]]) 2|25,000 [[Coins]]|50,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|
|Diamond Chest|Defeat Boss; Pass Class Milestone ([[Class Milestones]]) 2|50,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|
|Emerald Chest|Defeat Boss; Pass Class Milestone ([[Class Milestones]]) 2; Score 230+ (A (Score between 230 and 269 points.) ([[Dungeon Score]]) Rank) or Floor VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]]) Completion|100,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|500,000 [[Coins]]|500,000 [[Coins]]|
|Obsidian Chest|Defeat Boss; Pass Class Milestone ([[Class Milestones]]) 2; Score 270+ (S (Score between 270 and 299 points.) ([[Dungeon Score]]) Rank) or Floor VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]]) Completion|250,000 [[Coins]]|500,000 [[Coins]]|1,000,000 [[Coins]]|1,000,000 [[Coins]]|
|Bedrock Chest|Defeat Boss; Pass Class Milestone ([[Class Milestones]]) 2; Score 300+ (S+ (Score above 300 points.) ([[Dungeon Score]]) Rank)||||2,000,000 [[Coins]]|
## Item Upgrades
[[Weapons]] and [[Armor]] obtained from chests have a chance to gain different upgrades after being purchased.
|Upgrade|Chance Normal|Chance Master Mode|
|-|-|-|
|Rarity Upgrade ([[Recombobulator 3000]])|1%|1%|
|Growth VI ([[Growth]])|1%|1%|
|Protection VI ([[Protection]])|1%|1%|
|1 Star ([[Star Upgrades]]) (✪)|10%|20%|
|2 Stars ([[Star Upgrades]]) (✪✪)|5%|10%|
|3 Stars ([[Star Upgrades]]) (✪✪✪)|3%|6%|
|4 Stars ([[Star Upgrades]]) (✪✪✪✪)|2%|4%|
|5 Stars ([[Star Upgrades]]) (✪✪✪✪✪)|1%|2%|
## Loot Rolling Process
Dungeon Reward Chests are rolled using a system based on two important types of numbers: weight and quality.
When a chest first spawns, a chest quality value is generated for it, based on its loot table's starting quality and various upgrades the player may have. This value acts like a currency, as every item in the loot table also has quality cost.
Each item also has a value called weight, which determines its relative chance to be rolled for out of all eligible items. For example, if the weight of all items adds up to 100, an item with a weight of 10 has a 10% chance to be rolled. Items are not eligible if they've already been rolled or there is not enough leftover quality. [[Essence]] entries with non-zero weight and quality can roll multiple times.
Entries will repeatedly roll until there are no more eligible entries leftover. Then, if existent, the [[Essence]] entry with 1 quality and 0 weight will repeatedly roll, until all the leftover quality has been depleted. Finally, guaranteed entries will be added, such as [[Undead Essence]], [[Wither Essence]], and sometimes certain [[Attributes|Shards]].
Any possible rare [[Dyes]] and [[Music Discs]] roll after these steps are completed.
### Chest Quality
Each chest on every floor has a base chest quality. This is used to determine the items the player receives in the chest.
|Floor|Wood|Gold|Diamond|Emerald|Obsidian|Bedrock|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Floor I ([[The Catacombs - Floor I]])|110|160|200|240|300||
|Floor II ([[The Catacombs - Floor II]])|120|170|210|250|320||
|Floor III ([[The Catacombs - Floor III]])|125|180|220|260|330||
|Floor IV ([[The Catacombs - Floor IV]])|125|180|220|260|330||
|Floor V ([[The Catacombs - Floor V]])|125|180|220|260|330|370|
|Floor VI ([[The Catacombs - Floor VI]])|125|180|220|260|330|370|
|Floor VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]])|125|180|220|260|330|370|
|Floor I ([[The Catacombs - Floor I]]) (Master Mode)|120|170|215|255|320||
|Floor II ([[The Catacombs - Floor II]]) (Master Mode)|130|180|225|265|340||

#### Quality Upgrades
Chest quality can be buffed through multiple methods. This can increase the chance for certain items to appear, due to them gaining more opportunities to be rolled.
|Upgrade|Bonus|Note|
|-|-|-|
|Earning S+ ([[Dungeon Score]])|1.05x||
|Treasure Accessories; (Highest Tier) [[Treasure Artifact]]|1.03x|All apply twice|
|Treasure Accessories; (Highest Tier) [[Treasure Ring]]|1.02x|All apply twice|
|Treasure Accessories; (Highest Tier) [[Treasure Talisman]]|1.01x|All apply twice|
|Boss Luck Perk ([[Essence Shops]])|+1/3/5/10|All apply twice|
|Catacombs Box Attribute ([[Hideongeon Shard]])|+1-10 (or up to +13 with the buffs below)|All apply twice|
|Echo of Boxes Attribute ([[Cuboa Shard]])|Buffs the Catacombs Box Attribute ([[Hideongeon Shard]]) by 2-20%|All apply twice|
|Echo of Echoes Attribute ([[Tiamat Shard]])|Buffs the Echo of Boxes Attribute ([[Hideongeon Shard]]) by 5-50%|All apply twice|
#### Quality Formula
- InitialQuality=⌊BaseChestQuality×SPlusMultiplier⌋ IntendedQuality=⌊(InitialQuality×T)+B+C⌉ ActualQuality=⌊(IntendedQuality×T)+B+C⌉
where:
- T = The multiplier from [[Treasure Talisman]], [[Treasure Ring]], or [[Treasure Artifact]]
- B = The added bonus from the [[Essence Shops|Boss Luck Perk]]
- C = The added bonus from the [[Hideongeon Shard|Catacombs Box Attribute]]
Note: ⌊x⌋ indicates rounding down, while ⌊x⌉ indicates rounding to the nearest integer (.5 rounded up).

## Bugs
- The modifiers for the [[Treasure Talisman]], [[Treasure Ring]], [[Treasure Artifact]], [[Essence Shops|Boss Luck Perk]] perk and [[Hideongeon Shard|Catacombs Box Attribute]] all apply twice. This is due to the quality formula running once and being saved to a field, which can be seen in the API when looking at chest data, and then again using that same field as an input.
  - This also means the API's chest data shows a different quality value than the actual quality value used to roll the loot.

# Gear Score
Categories: Catacombs, Dungeons, Reforging, Enchants
[Gear Score]
Gear Score is an arbitrary estimate of how powerful [[Dungeons|Dungeon]] Armor and Weapons are. It allows players to compare two different Dungeon items without having to take all individual stats into account.
[[Reforging|Reforges]], [[Enchantments]], [[Star Upgrades]], and all item modifiers (including but not limited to [[Recombobulator 3000]], [[Hot Potato Book|Hot]] and [[Fuming Potato Book|Fuming Potato Books]], [[Master Star|Master Stars]], etc.) are taken into account when calculating Gear Score and will increase this stat. It has no effect on the stats of an item and is capped at 5000.
## Calculating
Points of stats and levels of enchantments contribute towards a Dungeon Item's Gear Score.
|Stat|Score|
|-|-|
|[[Health]]|1|
|[[Defense]]|2|
|[[Strength]]|1|
|[[Intelligence]]|0.5|
|[[Damage]]|1|
|[[Ferocity]]|1|
|[[Crit Chance]]|2|
|[[Crit Damage]]|0.8|
|[[Ability Damage]]|1|
|[[Attack Speed]]|2|
|[[Magic Find]]|1|
|Enchantment|Score|
|-|-|
|[[Thorns]]|2|
|[[Sharpness]]|2|
|[[Critical]]|2|

# Wither Key
Categories: Catacombs, Dungeons
[Wither Key]
A Wither Key is an item that can be obtained in the [[Catacombs]].
A Wither Key can used to open a Wither Doors in the [[Catacombs]]. The Wither Key is dropped by either a [[Minibosses|miniboss]], such as the [[Lost Adventurer]] or [[Angry Archaeologist]], or when all [[Mob|Mobs]] with a star have been slain.
Clicking a Wither Door with the Wither Key consumes the Wither Key and opens the door. The Wither Key is usable by any person within the dungeon, regardless of whether or not they are the player who obtained it.
## Trivia
- The Wither Key and [[Wither Essence]] use the same texture.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:

|Jul 7, 2020|0.8|Added Wither Key.|

### Boss Collection
An Adaptive Chestplate can be obtained once as a reward from [[The Professor|The Professor IV (4)]], after defeating [[The Professor]] 150 times.
Adaptive Leggings can be obtained once as a reward from [[The Professor|The Professor II (2)]], after defeating [[The Professor]] 50 times.

[Effects]
[Full Set Bonus: Efficient training; Every 5 Catacombs levels, this armor piece gains +2% stats.]
While inside the [[Catacombs]], each piece of Adaptive Armor gains increased stats depending on the user's selected [[Classes|Class]].
- [[Berserk|Berserks]] gain +20 [[Strength]].
- [[Healer|Healers]] gain +5 [[Mending]] and +40 [[Health]].
- [[Mage|Mages]] gain +50 [[Intelligence]].
- [[Tank|Tanks]] gain +30 [[Defense]]: additionally, damage taken is reduced by 5% for each piece of Adaptive Armor worn if the user is hit by the same enemy within 10s.
- [[Archer|Archers]] gain +5 [[Crit Chance]] and +15 [[Crit Damage]].
## Upgrading
Adaptive Armor can be upgraded into Adaptive Armor using 8 [[Scarf Fragment|Scarf Fragments]] to buff its stats, granting:
- +10 [[Health]]
- +10 [[Defense]]
- ☤ Defensive [[Gemstone Slot|Gemstone Slots]] become ⚔ Combat [[Gemstone Slot|Gemstone Slots]]
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Adaptive Helmet ([[Adaptive Armor]])|None|1x Adaptive Helmet ([[Adaptive Armor]]) 8x [[Scarf Fragment]]|Recipe: [[Scarf Fragment]] ×8 + [[Adaptive Armor]] → [[Adaptive Armor]]|
|Adaptive Chestplate ([[Adaptive Armor]])|None|1x Adaptive Chestplate ([[Adaptive Armor]]) 8x [[Scarf Fragment]]|Recipe: [[Scarf Fragment]] ×8 + [[Adaptive Armor]] → [[Adaptive Armor]]|
|Adaptive Leggings ([[Adaptive Armor]])|None|1x Adaptive Leggings ([[Adaptive Armor]]) 8x [[Scarf Fragment]]|Recipe: [[Scarf Fragment]] ×8 + [[Adaptive Armor]] → [[Adaptive Armor]]|
|Adaptive Boots ([[Adaptive Armor]])|None|1x Adaptive Boots ([[Adaptive Armor]]) 8x [[Scarf Fragment]]|Recipe: [[Scarf Fragment]] ×8 + [[Adaptive Armor]] → [[Adaptive Armor]]|

## Tips
- Despite its high [[Effective Health]], Adaptive Armor may be less worthwhile than it appears when compared to armor sets obtained on the same floor. For higher floors, [[Shadow Assassin Armor]], while being more difficult to obtain, grants higher [[Strength]] and [[Crit Damage]].
## Trivia
- Adaptive Armor and the [[Adaptive Blade]] are references to League of Legends' Adaptive Armor and Adaptive Helm.

Item ID: ULTIMATE_BANK
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Bank I Saves 10% of your coins on death.; Additionally, enemies drop +0.5 coins when killed.; You can only have 1 Ultimate Enchantment on an item!; Applicable on: Armor Apply Cost: 50 Exp Levels; Requires Enchanting 16 to apply.; COMMON
Bank is an Ultimate [[Ultimate Enchantments|Enchantment]] that decreases the [[Coins]] lost on death and gives coins when killing enemies. It can be applied to [[Armor]], starting at [[Enchanting|Enchanting XVI (16)]].
## Obtaining
|Enchantment Tier|Enchantment Source|
|-|-|
|Bank I|Drop from the [[Catacombs]] Floor 1 - Floor 7 Dungeon Reward Chests ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|
|Bank II|Drop from the [[Catacombs]] Floor 3 - Floor 7 Dungeon Reward Chests ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|
|Bank III|Drop from the [[Catacombs]] Floor 5 - Floor 7 Dungeon Reward Chests ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|
[[Enchanted Book]] (Bank) can be combined on an [[Anvil]] up to Bank V.

### Bazaar
The Bank [[Ultimate Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be traded through the [[Bazaar]].
|Enchantment|Tier|
|-|-|
|Bank|I|
|Bank|II|
|Bank|III|
|Bank|IV|
|Bank|V|
## Ability
Bank decreases [[Coins]] lost on death, stacking additively. It also gives 0.5 [[Coins]] - 2.5 [[Coins]] when the player kills a enemy.
|Tier|Bonus|
|-|-|
|Ⅰ|10% less [[Coins]] lost on death. Gives 0.5 [[Coins]] to the player when a mob is killed by them.|
|Ⅱ|20% less [[Coins]] lost on death. Gives 1 Coin ([[Coins]]) to the player when a mob is killed by them.|
|Ⅲ|30% less [[Coins]] lost on death. Gives 1.5 [[Coins]] to the player when a mob is killed by them.|
|Ⅳ|40% less [[Coins]] lost on death. Gives 2 [[Coins]] to the player when a mob is killed by them.|
|Ⅴ|50% less [[Coins]] lost on death. Gives 2.5 [[Coins]] to the player when a mob is killed by them.|
## Applying
The Bank [[Ultimate Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be applied to Armor. It requires [[Enchanting|Enchanting XVI (16)]] to apply.
|Tier|Cost|
|-|-|
|Ⅰ|50 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅱ|100 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅲ|150 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅳ|200 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅴ|250 [[Experience]]|

# Combo
Categories: Catacombs Loot, Enchantments
[Combo]
Type: Enchantment ([[Enchantments]])
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])-UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Enchantment Requirements]
Minimum Level: I
Maximum Level: V
Applied To: Melee Weapons
Req. [[Enchanting]] Level: Enchanting XXIV (24) ([[Enchanting]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ENCHANTMENT_ULTIMATE_COMBO
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Combo I Increases your damage by +1% per kill up to 2 kills within 2s.; You can only have 1 Ultimate Enchantment on an item!; Applicable on: Melee Weapon Apply Cost: 50 Exp Levels; Requires Enchanting 24 to apply.; COMMON
Combo is an Ultimate [[Ultimate Enchantments|Enchantment]] that increases [[Damage]] per kill. It can be applied to [[Swords]], [[Longswords]], and the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]], starting at [[Enchanting|Enchanting XXIV (24)]].
## Obtaining
The Combo [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be obtained in various ways:
|Enchantment Tier|Enchantment Source|
|-|-|
|Combo I|Drop from the [[Catacombs]] Floor 1 - Floor 7 Dungeon Reward Chests ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|
|Combo II|Drop from the [[Catacombs]] Floor 5 - Floor 7 Dungeon Reward Chests ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|
[[Enchanted Book]] (Combo) can be combined on an [[Anvil]] up to Combo V.

The Combo [[Ultimate Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be traded through the [[Bazaar]].
|Enchantment|Tier|
|-|-|
|Combo|I|
|Combo|II|
|Combo|III|
|Combo|IV|
|Combo|V|
## Ability
Increases your additive [[Damage]] by +1-5% per kill up to 2-10 kills within 2-10s.
|Tier|Damage Bonus (Additive)|Kill Cap|Duration|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ|+1%|2|2s|
|Ⅱ|+2%|4|4s|
|Ⅲ|+3%|6|6s|
|Ⅳ|+4%|8|8s|
|Ⅴ|+5%|10|10s|
## Applying
The Combo [[Ultimate Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be applied to any [[Swords|Sword]]. It requires [[Enchanting|Enchanting XXIV (24)]] to apply.
|Tier|Cost|
|-|-|
|Ⅰ|50 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅱ|100 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅲ|150 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅳ|200 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅴ|250 [[Experience]]|

# Feather Falling
Categories: Catacombs Loot, Enchantments
[Feather Falling]
Type: Enchantment ([[Enchantments]])
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])-MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])
[Enchantment Requirements]
Minimum Level: I
Maximum Level: X
Applied To: [[Boots]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ENCHANTMENT_FEATHER_FALLING
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Feather Falling I Increases how high you can fall before taking fall damage by 5 and reduces fall damage by 5%.; Applicable on: Boots Apply Cost: 10 Exp Levels; COMMON
Feather Falling is an [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] that reduces [[Damage Calculation|Fall Damage]]. It can be applied to [[Boots]].
## Obtaining
The Feather Falling [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be obtained in various ways:
|Enchantment Tier|Enchantment Source|
|-|-|
|Feather Falling I–V|Enchanting Table ([[Enchantment Table]])|
|Feather Falling VI|Drop from the [[Catacombs]] Floor 1 - Floor 7 Dungeon Reward Chests ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|
|Feather Falling VII|Drop from the [[Catacombs]] Floor 7 Dungeon Reward Chests ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|
[[Enchanted Book]] (Feather Falling VI–IX) can be combined on an [[Anvil]] up to Feather Falling X.

## Ability
Feather Falling increases the height the player can fall before taking [[Damage Calculation|Fall Damage]] and reduces [[Damage Calculation|Fall Damage]].
|Tier|Height|Reduction|
|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ|5|5%|
|Ⅱ|10|10%|
|Ⅲ|15|15%|
|Ⅳ|20|20%|
|Ⅴ|25|25%|
|Ⅵ|30|30%|
|Ⅶ|35|35%|
|Ⅷ|40|40%|
|Ⅸ|45|45%|
|Ⅹ|50|50%|
## Applying
Feather Falling can be applied to [[Boots]]. Unlocking [[Feather|Feather II (2)]] grants a -25% [[Experience]] discount on Feather Falling.
|Tier|Cost|
|-|-|

# Infinite Quiver
Categories: Catacombs Loot, Enchantments
[Infinite Quiver]
Type: Enchantment ([[Enchantments]])
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])-MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])
[Enchantment Requirements]
Minimum Level: I
Maximum Level: X
Applied To: Bow ([[Bows]])
Req. [[Enchanting]] Level: Enchanting II (2) ([[Enchanting]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ENCHANTMENT_INFINITE_QUIVER
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Infinite Quiver I Saves arrows 3% of the time when you fire your bow.; Applicable on: Bow Apply Cost: 10 Exp Levels; Requires Enchanting 2 to apply.; COMMON
Infinite Quiver is an [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] that grants a chance to save an arrow when shooting a bow. It can be applied to [[Bows]], starting at [[Enchanting|Enchanting II (2)]].
## Obtaining
The Infinite Quiver [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be obtained in various ways:
|Enchantment Tier|Enchantment Source|
|-|-|
|Infinite Quiver I–V|Enchanting Table ([[Enchantment Table]])|
|Infinite Quiver VI|Drop from the [[Catacombs]] Floor 1 - Floor 7 Dungeon Reward Chests ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|
|Infinite Quiver VII|Drop from the [[Catacombs]] Floor 7 Dungeon Reward Chests ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|
[[Enchanted Book]] (Infinite Quiver VI–IX) can be combined on an [[Anvil]] up to Infinite Quiver X.

## Ability
Infinite Quiver grants a chance to save an arrow when shooting a bow.
|Tier|Chance|
|-|-|
|Ⅰ|3%|
|Ⅱ|6%|
|Ⅲ|9%|
|Ⅳ|12%|
|Ⅴ|15%|
|Ⅵ|18%|
|Ⅶ|21%|
|Ⅷ|24%|
|Ⅸ|27%|
|Ⅹ|30%|
## Applying
Infinite Quiver can be applied to [[Bows]]. It requires [[Enchanting|Enchanting II (2)]] to apply. Unlocking [[String|String VI (6)]] grants a -25% [[Experience]] discount on Infinite Quiver.
|Tier|Cost|
|-|-|
|Ⅰ|10 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅱ|15 [[Experience]]|

## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Infinite Quiver I–V.|
|Jul 9, 2019|0.6|Changed Infinite Quiver I crafting recipe to Infinite Quiver II and made it 2 times more expensive.|
|Oct 14, 2019|[[Bows]] with Infinite Quiver now deal damage correctly.|
|Dec 3, 2019|Fixed Mosquito Bow ([[Mosquito Shortbow]]) and Scorpion Bow ([[Spider Shortbow]]) shots being stolen by Infinite Quiver.|
|Jul 7, 2020|0.8|Added Infinite Quiver VI as a drop from [[Catacombs]].|
|Jul 10, 2020|Infinite Quiver VI can now be combined on an [[Anvil]] up to Infinite Quiver X.|
|Nov 17, 2020|0.9.13|Infinite Quiver VII can now drop from Floor 7 ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]]) of [[Catacombs]].|
|Feb 16, 2021|0.11.1|Fixed Infinite Quiver X consuming arrows.|
|Sep 7, 2021|Nerfed chance to save arrows from 10% to 3% per tier.|
|Feb 6, 2024|0.19.11|String VI (6) ([[String]]) now grants a -25% experience discount on Infinite Quiver, instead of enchantment book recipe.|
|Oct 15, 2025|0.23.5|Sneaking no longer disables Infinite Quiver.|

### Combining
[[Enchanted Book]] (Last Stand) can be combined on an [[Anvil]] up to Last Stand V.
### Bazaar
The Last Stand [[Ultimate Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be traded through the [[Bazaar]].
|Enchantment|Tier|
|-|-|
|Last Stand|I|
|Last Stand|II|
|Last Stand|III|
|Last Stand|IV|
|Last Stand|V|
## Ability
Last Stand increases [[Defense]] for 10s and regenerates [[Vitality]] when falling below 40% [[Health]] with 30s cooldown.
|Tier|[[Defense]]|[[Vitality]]|
|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ|2.5% [[Defense]]|2 [[Vitality]]|
|Ⅱ|5% [[Defense]]|4 [[Vitality]]|
|Ⅲ|7.5% [[Defense]]|6 [[Vitality]]|
|Ⅳ|10% [[Defense]]|8 [[Vitality]]|
|Ⅴ|12.5% [[Defense]]|10 [[Vitality]]|
## Applying
The Last Stand [[Ultimate Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be applied to [[Armor]]. It requires [[Enchanting|Enchanting XXX (30)]] to apply.
|Tier|Cost|
|-|-|
|Ⅰ|50 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅱ|100 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅲ|150 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅳ|200 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅴ|250 [[Experience]]|
## Tips
- Last Stand's effect is buffed by up to +50% by the [[Ankylosaurus Pet]].
- Last Stand will stack across multiple armor pieces.

Master Skulls are a line of [[Accessories]] that rarely drop in every Master [[Catacombs]] Floor. They buff [[Health]] and [[Strength]] by an increasing percentage while in Master Mode.
## Obtaining
### Dungeon Reward Chests
Master Skulls can drop from [[Dungeon Reward Chest]] in any floor in Master Mode Catacombs. Higher tier skulls drop from higher floors.

### Crafting
Each tier of Master Skull can be crafted from 4 of the tier below it.
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|4x Master Skull - Tier 1 ([[Master Skull]])|Recipe: [[Master Skull]] ×4 → [[Master Skull]]|
### Raffle of the Century
Master Skull Tier 7 can also be obtained as a MAJOR reward from [[Raffle of the Century]].
## Usage
Each tier of Master Skull boosts [[Health]] and [[Strength]] by a percentage in Master Mode only.
|Tier|Rarity|HP ([[Health]]) and Str ([[Strength]]) boost|
|-|-|-|
|1|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|1%|
|2|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|2%|
|3|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|3%|
|4|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|4%|
|5|RARE ([[Rarity]])|6%|
|6|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|8%|
|7|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|10%|
## Trivia
- It would take 4,096 (64 stacks) [[Master Skull|Master Skull - Tier 1]] to make a [[Master Skull|Master Skull - Tier 7]]
- The stat boost from different tiers used to stack, meaning it was beneficial to have one of each master skull in your [[Accessory Bag]] or inventory as it could have stacked up to +34% [[Health]] and [[Strength]]. This has been since fixed and it no longer works.
