Master Stars are [[Rarity|EPIC]] items which upgrade 5-[[Star Upgrades|star]] [[Dungeons|Dungeon]] items with Master Stars, increasing their stats within Master Mode. They are obtained from [[Catacombs]] Master Floors III-VII.
## Obtaining
### Dungeon Reward Chests
Master Stars are rare drops from [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor III]] to [[The Catacombs - Floor VII|Floor VII]]. The floor they drop in is 2 higher than the tier of star it is.

## Usage
Master Stars can be used in anvils with Dungeon items to change their stars to Master Stars, which appear as red stars at the end of the item's name. Each Master Star grants +5% stats of that item in Master Mode. They take experience levels to apply.
Effect of each Master Star:
|Type|Used On|Once Applied|Stat Boost|XP to Apply|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|First|✪✪✪✪✪|✪✪✪✪✪➊|5%|300 Exp ([[Experience]])|
|Second|✪✪✪✪✪➊|✪✪✪✪✪➋|10%|350 Exp ([[Experience]])|
|Third|✪✪✪✪✪➋|✪✪✪✪✪➌|15%|400 Exp ([[Experience]])|
|Fourth|✪✪✪✪✪➌|✪✪✪✪✪➍|20%|450 Exp ([[Experience]])|
|Fifth|✪✪✪✪✪➍|✪✪✪✪✪➎|25%|500 Exp ([[Experience]])|
## Trivia
- It would cost a total of to apply all 5 stars, excluding [[Experience]].

### Boss Collection
A Necromancer Lord Helmet can be obtained once as a reward from [[Sadan|Sadan III (3)]], after defeating [[Sadan]] 150 times.
A Necromancer Lord Chestplate can be obtained once as a reward from [[Sadan|Sadan V (5)]], after defeating [[Sadan]] 500 times.
## Usage
Armor Set:
|Helmet|Chestplate|
|-|-|
|Necromancer Lord Helmet ([[Necromancer Lord Armor]]); [[Health]]: +200 [[Defense]]: +160 [[Intelligence]]: +15|Necromancer Lord Chestplate ([[Necromancer Lord Armor]]); [[Health]]: +300 [[Defense]]: +250 [[Intelligence]]: +10|
|Leggings|Boots|
|Necromancer Lord Leggings ([[Necromancer Lord Armor]]); [[Health]]: +250 [[Defense]]: +220 [[Intelligence]]: +10|Necromancer Lord Boots ([[Necromancer Lord Armor]]); [[Health]]: +180 [[Defense]]: +150 [[Intelligence]]: +10|
[Total]
[Necromancer Lord Armor Health: +930 Defense: +780 Intelligence: +45]
[Effects]
[Full Set Bonus: Soul Whisper; Increase the damage of your necromancer summoned mobs by +20%.]
Necromancer Lord Armor primarily grants a lot of bonuses to [[Necromancy]] through its Soul Whisper full set bonus, which increases the damage of summoned mobs by 20% and allows the wearer to store and summon 1 additional soul in their necromancer weapon: this includes the [[Reaper Scythe]], [[Summoning Ring]], and [[Necromancer Sword]].

- The Necromancer Lord Helmet resembles Duskull from Pokémon.
- The Necromancer Lord Chestplate is one of 9 armor pieces which have the darkest color with a hex color code of ⁠ #000000. The others are [[Bat Person Armor]], [[Obsidian Chestplate]], [[Squid Boots]], [[Tarantula Armor]], [[Shadow Assassin Armor]], [[Rancher's Boots]], [[Snorkeling Armor]] and [[Wither Armor]].

Wither Scrolls are [[Ability Scrolls]] obtained from [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]] that can be applied to a [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)|Necron's Blade]] and its variants; the [[Hyperion]], [[Astraea]], [[Valkyrie]], and [[Scylla]].
## Obtaining
### Dungeon Reward Chests
Wither Scrolls are rare rewards from [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]].

## Usage
Wither Scrolls can be applied to the following items:
- [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)|Necron's Blade]]
- [[Hyperion]]
- [[Astraea]]
- [[Valkyrie]]
- [[Scylla]]
There are 3 types of Wither Scrolls: [[Necron's Blade Scrolls|Implosion]], [[Necron's Blade Scrolls|Shadow Warp]], and [[Necron's Blade Scrolls|Wither Shield]]. Applying all 3 Scrolls on a Wither Blade will replace the abilities with Wither Impact, which is a combination of all 3 abilities, which also significantly reduces the ability cooldowns from 10 seconds to 0.15 seconds.
|Slot|Name|Description|
|-|-|-|
|[[Necron's Blade Scrolls]]|Implosion|Deals 10,000 [[Damage]] to all mobs in a 6 block radius.; Mana Cost: 300; Cooldown: 10s; Intelligence Scaling ([[Damage Calculation]]): 0.3|
|[[Necron's Blade Scrolls]]|Wither Shield|Reduces [[Damage]] taken by 10% for 5 seconds. Also grants an absorption shield that scales based on your [[Catacombs]] Level.; Mana Cost: 150; Vitality Cost: 50; Cooldown: 10s|
|[[Necron's Blade Scrolls]]|Shadow Warp|Creates a spatial distortion 10 blocks ahead of the player that sucks all enemies around it: using this ability again within 5 seconds detonates the warp and deals 10,000 [[Damage]] to all mobs within.; Mana Cost: 300; Cooldown: 10s; Intelligence Scaling ([[Damage Calculation]]): 0.3|
The amount of [[Absorption]] gained from Wither Shield and Wither Impact when activating Wither Shield is based on the player's [[Catacombs]] level.
Absorption=(12+CatacombsLevel*0.32)*50
This results in a minimum of 984 [[Absorption]] at [[Catacombs]] Level 24, due to that being required to unlock the [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)]] and its upgrades. Each level grants an additional +16 [[Absorption]], up to 1,400 [[Absorption]]. After 5 seconds of having the [[Absorption]] shield active, [[Vitality]] is refunded based on how much of it wasn't used.
|Catacombs Level|Absorption|
|-|-|
|24|984 [[Absorption]]|
|25|1,000 [[Absorption]]|
|30|1,080 [[Absorption]]|
|35|1,160 [[Absorption]]|
|40|1,240 [[Absorption]]|
|45|1,320 [[Absorption]]|
|50|1,400 [[Absorption]]|
If all 3 scrolls are applied, the abilities are replaced with Wither Impact.
|Slot|Name|Description|
|-|-|-|
|[[Necron's Blade Scrolls]]|Wither Impact|Teleports 10 blocks ahead of you dealing 10,000 [[Damage]] damage to nearby enemies within a 6 block radius. Also reduces your [[Damage]] taken by 10% and grants an absorption shield for 5 seconds.; Mana Cost: 300; Vitality Cost: 50; Cooldown: 0.15s; Intelligence Scaling ([[Damage Calculation]]): 0.3|
If the player does not have 50 [[Vitality]], then the Wither Shield ability is not activated with this.
## Trivia
- Shadow Warp's teleportation is disabled during the boss fight of [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]], and the teleportation aspects of Wither Impact do not work during the boss fight.
- Wither Impact was nerfed with a 3 tick cooldown due to autoclickers.
- The [[Necron's Blade Scrolls|Ultimate Wither Scroll]] is an unobtainable item that grants the Wither Impact ability, which is given to 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] 56ms.
## History

|Jun 10, 2026|Changed Wither Shield to grant [[Absorption]] based on the player's [[Catacombs]] level, rather than granting 150% of the player's [[Crit Chance]] as [[Absorption]], and then converting it to [[Health]] after 5 seconds.|
|Jun 10, 2026|Wither Shield now grants a [[Vitality]] refund after 5 seconds based on how much of the [[Absorption]] shield is used.|
|Aug 25, 2026|Adjusted category of Implosion, Shadow Warp and Wither Shield from DUNGEON ITEM ([[Dungeon Items]]) → ABILITY SCROLL ([[Ability Scrolls]]).|

### Bazaar
The No Pain No Gain [[Ultimate Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be traded through the [[Bazaar]].
|Enchantment|Tier|
|-|-|
|No Pain No Gain|I|
|No Pain No Gain|II|
|No Pain No Gain|III|
|No Pain No Gain|IV|
|No Pain No Gain|V|
## Ability
Gain 20% per level to gain 10 experience orbs every time you take hits from mobs.
|Tier|Bonus|
|-|-|
|Ⅰ|20%|
|Ⅱ|40%|
|Ⅲ|60%|
|Ⅳ|80%|
|Ⅴ|100%|
## Applying
The No Pain No Gain [[Ultimate Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be applied to any [[Armor|Armor Piece]]. It requires [[Enchanting|Enchanting XXIX (29)]] to apply.
|Tier|Cost|
|-|-|
|Ⅰ|45 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅱ|91 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅲ|136 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅳ|179 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅴ|223 [[Experience]]|

# Rejuvenate
Categories: Catacombs Loot, Enchantments
[Rejuvenate]
Type: Enchantment ([[Enchantments]])
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])-UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Enchantment Requirements]
Minimum Level: I
Maximum Level: V
Applied To: [[Armor]]
Req. [[Enchanting]] Level: Enchanting X (10) ([[Enchanting]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ENCHANTMENT_REJUVENATE
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Rejuvenate I Grants +1 Health Regen.; Applicable on: Armor Apply Cost: 10 Exp Levels; Requires Enchanting 10 to apply.; COMMON
Rejuvenate is an [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] that grants [[Health Regen]]. It can be applied to [[Armor]], and is mutually exclusive with [[Respite]].
## Obtaining
The Rejuvenate [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be obtained in various ways:
|Enchantment Tier|Enchantment Source|
|-|-|
|Rejuvenate I|Drop from the [[Catacombs]] Floor 1 - Floor 7 Dungeon Reward Chests ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|
|Rejuvenate II|Drop from the [[Catacombs]] Floor 3 - Floor 7 Dungeon Reward Chests ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|
|Rejuvenate III|Drop from the [[Catacombs]] Floor 5 - Floor 7 Dungeon Reward Chests ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|
[[Enchanted Book]] (Rejuvenate) can be combined on an [[Anvil]] up to Rejuvenate V.

# Rend
Categories: Catacombs Loot, Enchantments
[Rend]
Type: Enchantment ([[Enchantments]])
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])-UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Enchantment Requirements]
Minimum Level: I
Maximum Level: V
Req. [[Enchanting]] Level: Enchanting XXXII (32) ([[Enchanting]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ENCHANTMENT_ULTIMATE_REND
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Rend I Use Left Click ability to rip your arrows out of nearby enemies. Each arrow deals 5% of your last critical shot on the target, up to 5 arrows.; 2s Cooldown.; You can only have 1 Ultimate Enchantment on an item!; Applicable on: Bow Apply Cost: 50 Exp Levels; Requires Enchanting 32 to apply.; COMMON
Rend is an Ultimate [[Ultimate Enchantments|Enchantment]] that lets the [[Player]] rip arrows out of their enemies, dealing a percentage of the last [[Crit Damage]] shot.
## Obtaining
The Rend [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be obtained in various ways:
|Enchantment Tier|Enchantment Source|
|-|-|
|Rend I|Drop from the [[Catacombs]] Floor 4 Dungeon Reward Chests ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|
|Rend II|Drop from the [[Catacombs]] Floor 4 Dungeon Reward Chests ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|
[[Enchanted Book]] (Rend) can be combined on an [[Anvil]] up to Rend V.

The Rend [[Ultimate Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be traded through the [[Bazaar]].
|Enchantment|Tier|
|-|-|
|Rend|I|
|Rend|II|
|Rend|III|
|Rend|IV|
|Rend|V|
## Ability
Use Left Click ability to rip your arrows out of nearby enemies. Each arrow deals 5-25% of your last critical shot on the target, up to 5 arrows. 2s Cooldown.
|Tier|Bonus|
|-|-|
|Ⅰ|5%|
|Ⅱ|10%|
|Ⅲ|15%|
|Ⅳ|20%|
|Ⅴ|25%|
## Applying
The Rend [[Ultimate Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be applied to any [[Bows|Bow]]. It requires [[Enchanting|Enchanting XXXII (32)]] to apply.
|Tier|Cost|
|-|-|
|Ⅰ|45 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅱ|91 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅲ|136 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅳ|179 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅴ|223 [[Experience]]|
## Trivia
- Rend is inspired by a League of Legends character Kalista. Kalista's third ability and the enchant have the same name and functions similarly.
  - Rend is also directly related to another [[Hypixel]] mini-game, Mega Walls. It functions in the same way as Renegade's "Rend" ability.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jul 30, 2020|Added Rend I.|
|Jul 30, 2020|Added Rend II.|
|Jul 30, 2020|Added Rend III.|
|Jul 30, 2020|Added Rend IV.|
|Jul 30, 2020|Added Rend V.|
|Aug 13, 2021|Rend now only takes into account crits from the Rend Bow ([[Bows]]) itself.|
|Aug 13, 2021|Rend only counts the shots on the mob which is being 'Rended'.|

Shadow Assassin Armor's full set bonus, Shadow Assassin, grants +1 [[Strength]] per enemy killed in a [[Catacombs]] run, resetting at the beginning of every run. Additionally, each piece of Shadow Assassin Armor grants the user a unique ability upon teleporting.
- The Shadow Assassin Helmet grants Invisibility and +20 [[Speed]].
- The Shadow Assassin Chestplate grants +10 [[Strength]] for 10s.
- The Shadow Assassin Leggings restores 1% of the user's maximum [[Health]], with a cooldown of 3s.
- The Shadow Assassin Boots restores +10 [[Mana]], with a cooldown of 3s.
[[Ender Pearl|Ender Pearls]], [[Silent Pearl|Silent Pearls]], the [[Aspect of the End]] and [[Aspect of the Void|Void]], the [[Etherwarp Conduit]], the [[Sinseeker Scythe]][Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)], the [[Silent Death]], the [[Shadow Fury]], and the Shadow Warp ability affect the different bonuses of each piece.
## Upgrading
Shadow Assassin Armor can be upgraded into Shadow Assassin Armor using 8 [[Livid Fragment|Livid Fragments]] to buff its stats, granting:
- +5 [[Strength]]
- +10 [[Health]]
- +1 [[Speed]]
- Replaces [[Gemstone|Jasper]] [[Gemstone Slot]] with a ⚔ Combat [[Gemstone Slot]]
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x Shadow Assassin Helmet ([[Shadow Assassin Armor]]) 8x [[Livid Fragment]]|Recipe: [[Livid Fragment]] ×8 + [[Shadow Assassin Armor]] → [[Shadow Assassin Armor]]|
|None|1x Shadow Assassin Chestplate ([[Shadow Assassin Armor]]) 8x [[Livid Fragment]]|Recipe: [[Livid Fragment]] ×8 + [[Shadow Assassin Armor]] → [[Shadow Assassin Armor]]|
|None|1x Shadow Assassin Leggings ([[Shadow Assassin Armor]]) 8x [[Livid Fragment]]|Recipe: [[Livid Fragment]] ×8 + [[Shadow Assassin Armor]] → [[Shadow Assassin Armor]]|
|None|1x Shadow Assassin Boots ([[Shadow Assassin Armor]]) 8x [[Livid Fragment]]|Recipe: [[Livid Fragment]] ×8 + [[Shadow Assassin Armor]] → [[Shadow Assassin Armor]]|

## Trivia
- The Shadow Assassin Armor is one of 9 armor pieces which have the darkest color with a hex color code of ⁠ #000000. The others are [[Bat Person Armor]], [[Obsidian Chestplate]], [[Squid Boots]], [[Tarantula Armor]], [[Rancher's Boots]], [[Necromancer Lord Armor|Necromancer Lord Chestplate]], [[Snorkeling Armor]] and [[Wither Armor]].
- There used to be a 1/10,000 chance of a piece of Shadow Assassin Armor being a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] piece, instead of an [[Rarity|EPIC]]. This is caused through 2 [[Recombobulator 3000|Recombobulator 3000s]] being applied, once upon viewing the chest, and a second upon opening the chest. It could then become [[Rarity|DIVINE]] (previously [[Rarity|SUPREME]]) if upgraded with [[Livid Fragment|Livid Fragments]].
- The Shadow Assassin Helmet is likely modelled after a Dark Hyrule Warrior.

# Spirit Pet
Aliases: Spirit
Categories: Catacombs Loot, Pets, Templates with deprecated argument names, Catacombs, Dungeons, Combat pets
[Spirit Pet]
Type: [[Combat]] Pet ([[Pets]])
Rarities: EPIC ([[Rarity]])–LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
Mob: Floating Head
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Rideable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PET
Pet ID: SPIRIT
Tooltip: Combat Pet; Intelligence: +1➡100; Speed: +0.3➡30; Spirit Assistance Spawns and assists you when you are a ghost in Dungeons.; Spirit Cooldowns Reduces the cooldown of your ghost abilities in dungeons by 5.45➡50%.; This pet's perks are active even when the pet is not summoned!; Right-click to add this pet to your pet menu!; EPIC
The Spirit Pet is a [[Combat]] [[Pets|Pet]] rarely obtained from [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] on [[The Catacombs - Floor IV]], which focuses on making the player stronger while they are a Ghost. The Pet is automatically activated when the player dies.
## Obtaining

## Bugs
- The Spirit Assistance perk automatically equips the Spirit Pet if the player disconnects from a [[Dungeons|Dungeon]].
  - This ignores [[Autopet Rules 2-Pack|Autopet Rules]].
## Tips
- The [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Spirit Pet]] Spirit Pet's ability, Half Life, is good for players with low survivability because it reduces the penalty for dying; however, it is recommended to improve one's stats and [[Accessory Power]] to prevent dying in the first place instead.
## Trivia
- The [[Spirit Pet]] Spirit Pet, [[Grandma Wolf Pet]], [[Kuudra Pet]], [[Bingo Pet]], [[Owl Pet]], and [[Parrot Pet]] are the only pets that grant perks to the player even when they are not equipped.
- [[Rarity|EPIC]] and [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Spirit Pet]] Spirit Pets used to have the Spirit Leap ability that, at Lvl 100, granted a 10% chance for [[Minibosses]] in dungeons to drop 5 [[Spirit Leap|Spirit Leaps]].

|Mar 30, 2021|0.11.3|Replaced Spirit Leap with Half Life to LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) Spirit Pet, which reduces the score penalty on the first death, every dungeon run.|

Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Ultimate Jerry I Increases the base damage of Aspect of the Jerry by 1000%.; You can only have 1 Ultimate Enchantment on an item!; Applicable on: Aspect of the Jerry, Signature Edition, Aspect of the Jerry Apply Cost: 50 Exp Levels; Requires Enchanting 18 to apply.; COMMON
Ultimate Jerry is an Ultimate [[Ultimate Enchantments|Enchantment]] that increases the base [[Damage]] of [[Aspect of the Jerry]].
## Obtaining
The Ultimate Jerry [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be obtained in various ways:
|Enchantment Tier|Enchantment Source|
|-|-|
|Ultimate Jerry I|Drop from the [[Catacombs]] Floor 1 - Floor 7 Dungeon Reward Chests ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|
|Ultimate Jerry II|Drop from the [[Catacombs]] Floor 3 - Floor 7 Dungeon Reward Chests ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|
|Ultimate Jerry III|Drop from the [[Catacombs]] Floor 5 - Floor 7 Dungeon Reward Chests ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|

### Bazaar
The Ultimate Jerry [[Ultimate Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be traded through the [[Bazaar]].
|Enchantment|Tier|
|-|-|
|Ultimate Jerry|I|
|Ultimate Jerry|II|
|Ultimate Jerry|III|
|Ultimate Jerry|IV|
|Ultimate Jerry|V|
## Ability
It increases the base [[Damage]] of [[Aspect of the Jerry]] by up to 5000%.
|Tier|Bonus|
|-|-|
|Ⅰ|1000% more base [[Damage]]|
|Ⅱ|2000% more base [[Damage]]|
|Ⅲ|3000% more base [[Damage]]|
|Ⅳ|4000% more base [[Damage]]|
|Ⅴ|5000% more base [[Damage]]|
## Applying
The Ultimate Jerry [[Ultimate Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be applied to [[Aspect of the Jerry]]. It requires [[Enchanting|Enchanting XVIII (18)]] to apply.
|Tier|Cost|
|-|-|
|Ⅰ|45 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅱ|91 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅲ|136 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅳ|179 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅴ|223 [[Experience]]|
## Trivia
- The loot ID for Ultimate Jerry in dungeon reward chests is master_jerry_ followed by the enchantment level.
  - This can be seen in the Hypixel Public API when querying the profile of a player who has recently obtained a chest with an Ultimate Jerry book.

# Ultimate Wise
Categories: Catacombs Loot, Enchantments
[Ultimate Wise]
Type: Enchantment ([[Enchantments]])
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])-UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Enchantment Requirements]
Maximum Level: V
Req. [[Enchanting]] Level: Enchanting XX (20) ([[Enchanting]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ENCHANTMENT_ULTIMATE_WISE
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Ultimate Wise I Reduces the ability mana cost of this item by 10%.; You can only have 1 Ultimate Enchantment on an item!; Applicable on: Held Item, Precursor Eye Apply Cost: 50 Exp Levels; Requires Enchanting 20 to apply.; COMMON
Ultimate Wise is an Ultimate [[Ultimate Enchantments|Enchantment]] that decreases [[Mana]] cost from Weapons, Tools, Fishing Rods, and Wands.
## Obtaining
The Ultimate Wise [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be obtained in various ways:
|Enchantment Tier|Enchantment Source|
|-|-|
|Ultimate Wise I|Drop from the [[Catacombs]] Floor 2 - Floor 7 Dungeon Reward Chests ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|
|Ultimate Wise II|Drop from the [[Catacombs]] Floor 5 - Floor 7 Dungeon Reward Chests ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|
[[Enchanted Book]] (Ultimate Wise) can be combined on an [[Anvil]] up to Ultimate Wise V.

## Ability
The Ultimate Wise [[Ultimate Enchantments|Enchantment]] decreases [[Mana]] cost by up to 50%.
|Tier|Bonus|
|-|-|
|Ⅰ|10% less [[Mana]] cost|
|Ⅱ|20% less [[Mana]] cost|
|Ⅲ|30% less [[Mana]] cost|
|Ⅳ|40% less [[Mana]] cost|
|Ⅴ|50% less [[Mana]] cost|
## Applying
The Ultimate Wise [[Ultimate Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be applied to Weapons, Tools, Fishing Rods, and Wands. It requires [[Enchanting|Enchanting XX (20)]] to apply.
|Tier|Cost|
|-|-|
|Ⅰ|50 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅱ|100 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅲ|150 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅳ|200 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅴ|250 [[Experience]]|

|Wisdom II|Drop from the [[Catacombs]] Floor 5 - Floor 7 Dungeon Reward Chests ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|
[[Enchanted Book]] (Wisdom) can be combined on an [[Anvil]] up to Wisdom V.

Tooltip: Gear Score: 440 (482); Health: +200 (+220); Defense: +100 (+110); Intelligence: +30 (+33); Reduces the damage you take from withers by 10%.; Full Set Bonus: Witherborn (0\ 4); Spawns a wither minion every 30; seconds up to a maximum 1 wither.; Your withers will travel to and explode on nearby enemies.; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires The Catacombs Floor VII Completion.; LEGENDARY DUNGEON HELMET
Wither Armor is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Armor|Dungeon Armor]] set that can be obtained from [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]].
## Obtaining
### Boss Collection
The Wither Helmet, Chestplate, and Leggings can be obtained as rewards through the [[Necron]] Collection.
- Wither Helmet: [[Necron|Necron III (3)]], after killing [[Necron]] 150 times.
- Wither Chestplate: [[Necron|Necron VI (6)]], after killing [[Necron]] 750 times.
- Wither Leggings: [[Necron|Necron V (5)]], after killing [[Necron]] 500 times.
### Dungeon Reward Chests
Wither Armor can be obtained from [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]]. When obtained, it has a chance to have 1-5 Stars, [[Growth|Growth VI]], [[Protection|Protection VI]], and/or a [[Recombobulator 3000]] applied.

### Crafting
A piece of Wither Armor can be upgraded into the corresponding piece of [[Storm's Armor]], [[Goldor's Armor]], [[Maxor's Armor]], or [[Necron's Armor]] by surrounding it with 8 of the respective [[Precursor Relic]].
- [[Storm's Armor]] is created with [[L.A.S.R.'s Eye]] and increases [[Intelligence]].
- [[Goldor's Armor]] is created with [[Jolly Pink Rock]] and increases [[Health]] and [[Defense]].
- [[Maxor's Armor]] is created with [[Bigfoot's Bola]] and increases [[Speed]] and [[Crit Damage]].
- [[Necron's Armor]] is created with [[Diamante's Handle]] and increases [[Strength]] and [[Crit Damage]].
Upgrading the armor preserves existing upgrades, including Stars, reforges, Hot Potato Books, and enchantments. Wither Armor variants can also be converted between one another using the appropriate Precursor Relic.

|-|-|-|-|
|Maxor's Helmet ([[Maxor's Armor]])|None|8 [[Bigfoot's Bola]] Wither Helmet ([[Wither Armor]]) variant|Recipe: [[Bigfoot's Bola]] ×8 + [[Wither Armor]]/[[Storm's Armor]]/[[Goldor's Armor]]/[[Necron's Armor]] → [[Maxor's Armor]]|
|Necron's Helmet ([[Necron's Armor]])|None|8 [[Diamante's Handle]] Wither Helmet ([[Wither Armor]]) variant|Recipe: [[Diamante's Handle]] ×8 + [[Wither Armor]]/[[Maxor's Armor]]/[[Storm's Armor]]/[[Goldor's Armor]] → [[Necron's Armor]]|

## Trivia
- When the armor was first released, crafting Wither Armor into an upgraded variant preserved its ⁠ #000000 color, creating rare black variants.
  - On March 1, 2022, an update temporarily reintroduced this behavior, causing the value of existing black variants to decrease.
    - This was patched within a few hours, but many additional black variants were created during that time.
      - Since the release of [[Pure Black Dye]], these variants are no longer exclusive, as the dye can recolor Leather Armor to ⁠ #000000 and costs 250,000 [[Bits]].
- [[Rarity|DIVINE]] (previously [[Rarity|SUPREME]]) Wither Armor and its variants were possible to obtain if the armor was glitched and recombobulated twice upon opening a [[Dungeon Reward Chest]].
- Donating any of the four Wither Armor variants to the Museum gives credit for Wither Armor, but donating Wither Armor does not give credit for any of the variants.
- You can disable the Witherborn full set bonus by clicking on the key bind assigned to your "Pick Block" button in the key binds settings.
## History

|Apr 18, 2023|0.18.3|Fixed the Witherborn full set bonus working as a ghost in [[Dungeons]].|
|Feb 6, 2024|0.19.11|The Witherborn ability no longer attempts to attack Garden Pests ([[Pests]]).|

Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: CAKE_COUNTER
Tooltip: Furniture; Counts and displays the number of century cakes eaten on your island.; EPIC
The Cake Counter is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Furniture]] item that can be used stats about [[Century Cakes]] on the [[Private Island]].
## Obtaining
The Cake Counter can be obtained as a minor reward from the [[Raffle of the Century]].
Cake Counter can also be chosen as a reward from [[Gift Bag of the Century]].
## Usage
The Cake Counter can be placed on the player's [[Private Island]] as a piece of [[Furniture]]. Above it are two lines of text, one that displays the number of [[Century Cakes]] eaten, and another that displays how many [[Cake Soul|Cake Souls]] have been obtained from them.
## Gallery
- A render of the cake counter, not including the text.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Sep 8, 2020|0.9|Added Cake Counter.|
|Feb 14, 2025|Removed 300 from the cake.|

Tooltip: Right-Click on a player on the RED team while holding this item to feed it to them!; Feeding a slice of cake to another player gives both of you a reward!; * Soulbound * X Rift-Transferable X SPECIAL
Cake Slice is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] item type only obtainable during [[Raffle of the Century]].
## Obtaining
Throughout the [[Raffle of the Century]], players can obtain various Cake Slices through Daily Quests, Raffle Milestones and Compensation Rewards. There are 5 Cake Slices, with each variant matching a team color.
## Usage
The player can give another player a Cake Slice to eat for both players to earn 1 [[Raffle of the Century|Raffle Ticket]], as well extra rewards including [[Coins]], extra [[Raffle of the Century|Raffle Tickets]], [[Token of the Century|Tokens of the Century]], [[Skills|Skill]] XP or a [[Century Cakes|Century Cake]].
Player can eat up to 25 other players Cake Slices every 2 hours. Player owned Cake Slices have no limit. [Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]

Tooltip: Commemorates the victory of the GREEN team in the Year ### celebrations.; Earned by: [player]; Your team: RED Contribution: # tickets; Taste of red velvet.; Memento acquirer name becomes hidden if traded.; SPECIAL MEMENTO
The Cake Slice of the Century is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] [[Mementos|Memento]].
## Obtaining
A Cake Slice of the Century is rewarded to the player at the end of the [[Raffle of the Century]] if they have joined a team by talking to [[Rezzus]]. The number of tickets the player has pooled and the name of the team they were on is displayed on the memento.
Additionally, the team the player was on affects the memento's lore and texture.
|Slot|Team|Lore|
|-|-|-|
|Cake Slice of the Century|RED|Taste of red velvet.|
|Cake Slice of the Century|BLUE|Taste of blueberry.|
|Cake Slice of the Century|GREEN|Taste of green lime.|
|Cake Slice of the Century|YELLOW|Taste of lemon cheesecake.|
|Cake Slice of the Century|PINK|Taste of strawberry.|
The GREEN team won the [[Raffle of the Century]] in SkyBlock Year 300, meaning all mementos will commemorate the victory of the team in the first line of its lore.
## History

|Sep 2, 2023|Added Cake Slice of the Century.|

Tooltip: Furniture; Furniture can be placed on your world. Some furniture is interactable while some are purely cosmetic.; Invite your friends to share a slice of cake! Eating this cake grants +10&#xE010; Health for 48 hours.; Obtained in Year [year].; UNCOMMON
Century Cakes are [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] items. They are placeable [[Furniture]] which give small [[Stats|Stat]] bonuses when consumed.
## Obtaining
Century Cakes can obtained via various methods:
- From [[Simon]] every 100 SkyBlock Years.
- During [[Raffle of the Century]] as a MINOR reward and as a consolation reward from the [[Raffle of the Century|The Big One]].
- By consuming the [[Complete Century Cake Bundle]] or the [[Random Century Cake Bundle]]
- By being chosen as a reward from a [[Gift Bag of the Century]].
## Mechanics
To activate a cake's effects, they must be right clicked while placed down on the [[Private Island]]. Cakes placed on others' Islands can grant their effects. Once placed down, they will never disappear, work as [[Furniture]] (but have their own furniture limit), and can be used endlessly.
There are 20 different types, each giving a different stat bonus for 48 hours. These 48 hours include offline time, similar to the [[Booster Cookie]], but they can be eaten again to reset the cooldown.
If the player has more than 8 hours of playtime, a Century Cake will have a 0.1% chance to drop a [[Cake Soul]] of its color when eaten. [[Cake Soul|Cake Souls]] are not obtainable while the stat boost from a Century Cake is still active.

|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Eating cake|Yum! You gain +# Stat for 48 hours!|
|Eating cake while stat boost is still active|Big Yum! You refresh +# Stat for 48 hours!|
## Trivia
- Each Century Cake's name and their respective effect is a reference to something related to Hypixel:
  - The [[Century Cakes|Crab-Colored Century Cake]] is a reference to the [[Crab Hat of Celebration]], which is also given out by [[Simon]]. It likely gives a [[Health]] bonus as most crabs are red, like the stat.
  - The [[Century Cakes|Pet Rock Century Cake]] is a reference to the [[Rock Pet]], which has a very high [[Defense]] stat.
  - The [[Century Cakes|Fruit Century Cake]] (formerly known as aPunch Century Cake) is a reference to 12[[Ranks|[ADMIN]]] aPunch. It gives +2 [[Strength]] due to [[Strength]] making punches stronger.
  - The [[Century Cakes|Potato-Style Century Cake]] is a reference to Technoblade and [[The Great Potato War]]. Its stat bonus references Technoblade's obsession with [[Speed]].
  - The [[Century Cakes|Barry Century Cake]] is a reference to the now-retired mayoral candidate [[Candidate Barry]], who was a wizard specializing in [[Intelligence]].
  - The [[Century Cakes|Sea Emperor Century Cake]] gives [[Sea Creature Chance]] due to the [[The Loch Emperor|Sea Creature]] it is named after.
  - The [[Century Cakes|Century Cake of the Next Dungeon Floor]] giving +1 [[Magic Find]] is a reference to many rare drops being found in [[Dungeons]]. It also teased the release of [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]].
  - The [[Century Cakes|Latest Update Century Cake]] giving +2 [[Ferocity]] is a reference to [[Ferocity]] being the most recently added stat in the game during the release of the Century Cakes.
  - The [[Century Cakes|Streamer's Century Cake]] giving +1 [[Pet Luck]] is a reference to streamers feeling luckier than regular players. It could also be a reference to 7Refraction, who has a popular clip of him having horrible luck with maxed [[Pet Luck]].
  - The [[Century Cakes|Century Cake of Hype]] grants +5 [[Mining Fortune]]. It possibly refers to the Hype Meter and the Hype Diamonds in the Prototype Lobby, and may be a memorial of that [[SkyBlock|Hypixel SkyBlock]] has received enough Hype Diamonds to be released.
  - The [[Century Cakes|Undead Century Cake]] giving [[Vitality]] is a reference to [[Zombie Slayer]], where many of the related items give healing.
  - The [[Century Cakes|ekaC yrutneC tfiR]] is a reference to the [[Mirrorverse]], an area in [[Rift Dimension|The Rift]] which features items with inverted names in one of its puzzles.
  - The [[Century Cakes|Corpse-Flavored Century Cake]] is a reference to the corpses found within [[Glacite Mineshafts]].
  - The [[Century Cakes|Starborn Century Cake]] may be a reference to the Starborn, who were mentioned by [[Loras]] as connected to the precursors and the [[Hunting]] skill in [[SkyBlock|SkyBlock's]] lore. It may also be a reference to the [[Starborn Shard]], one of the most expensive shards at the time of this century cake's release.
  - The [[Century Cakes|Long-Expired Century Cake]] is a reference to the fact that the Galatea update took an extremely long time to make and release, with the stat it grants being [[Sweep]], one of the stats introduced in the Galatea update.
  - The [[Century Cakes|Hephaestus' Century Cake]] references items from the Diana v2 update, namely the [[Hephaestus Anvil]] and the pet items crafted with it. [[Tracking]] as a stat is often associated with the [[Mythological Ritual]].
  - The remaining cakes seem to have no specific references to Hypixel, but are instead a reference to their color or their stat.
- The [[Century Cakes|Not-a-lie Century Cake]] is the only Century Cake that references something outside Hypixel, specifically the series Portal, where the player character Chell is promised Cake, but as hinted by secrets in the game, the promise, or the cake, is a lie. This is not to be confused with [[The Cake]] obtained from the [[Royal Resident]].
- The [[Century Cakes|Streamer's Century Cake]] is the only Century Cake that used to be [[Rarity|EPIC]] rather than [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]].
- Some islands that have every Century Cake include 5[[Ranks|[MVP+]]] Minikloon, 12[[Ranks|[ADMIN]]] Jayavarmen, 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] PortalHub, 5[[Ranks|[MVP+]]] cake_hub, 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] Tubbo_, and 12[[Ranks|[ADMIN]]] TimeDeo.
- When removing the [[Century Cakes|aPunch Century Cake]], the chat message displays the name as an APunch Century Cake, with the A capitalized, whereas placing it, and the item name, refers to it as an aPunch Century Cake.
- The [[Century Cakes|Streamer's Century Cake]] gives a purple [[Cake Soul]] rather than a magenta one, although it has the same color of [[Wool|Magenta Wool]].
- The [[Century Cakes|Pet Rock Century Cake]] used to give a green [[Cake Soul]] rather than a lime one, but this has been changed so [[Century Cakes|Undead Century Cake]] gives it instead.
- The [[Century Cakes|Century Cake of Hype]] and [[Century Cakes|Barry Century Cake]] used to have their colors of [[Cake Soul|Cake Souls]] swapped.
- The [[Century Cakes|Century Cake of Hype]] was unobtainable for a day when it was released.
- On the [[Alpha Hypixel Network]], before the Year 300 event, players could obtain cakes on year 299, while on every other occasion they were obtained on a year divisible by 100.
